# Changelog

One line per commit, newest phase first. Written by the orchestrator at the end of each phase.

## [Unreleased]

- `fix(desktop): the build works again: a CSS comment held a star-slash and closed itself early.` README 3.1 (scan-sweep adoption pass): the `.depth-pending` comment in app.css named `scenes/*/live.tsx`, so the minifier read the rest as a rule and `pnpm build` failed; the tests, lint and typecheck never build CSS, so the gate stayed green. Tests: styles/source-guards.test (no comment holds a star-slash of its own), seeded red then green.
- `fix(stage): the gauge's 96px and its dock-plus-44px are written once, as --gauge-top and --gauge-bottom.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, code-optimizer): the box and the mark's travel each spelled the numbers, so changing one left the mark off its rail; both now read the two tokens. `stage.css.test.ts` pins that each number appears once.
- `fix(depth): a hash the grammar did not write is rewritten to the place it resolved to when the window starts.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, state-coverage): `#/nowhere/at/all` opened the surface but stayed in the URL until the first move; `startDepth` now `replaceState`s the canonical form (an absent or canonical hash is left alone). `store.test.ts` pins both.
- `fix(stage): a crumb button is a 32px by text-plus-12px target, not the 16px of its text.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, mobile-specialist): `.stage__crumb button` had no padding; it holds 8px 6px and gives it back as margin, so the bar and the crumbs do not move, and its focus ring now wraps a target. `stage.css.test.ts` pins it.
- `fix(apps): an orb's focus ring is no longer cut off by its content-visibility box.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): `.apps__orbs > li` paints inside its own box and the orb fills it, so the 4px the ring reaches was clipped; the item holds 4px and gives it back as margin, as the chores grid does. The accounts card, entry and panel and the voice panel were checked and need nothing: the lifted card is the element itself, and the others have more padding than the ring reaches.
- `fix(scenes): a depth's lazy chunk shows a calm "Opening…" instead of a blank depth.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, state-coverage): the five `live.tsx` Suspense fallbacks were `null`; `.depth-pending` in app.css is silent for 150 ms, then fades in on opacity alone, holds 40vh so the view does not shift anything, and is hidden under Calm. No new import, so the first-paint graph is unchanged.
- `fix(accounts): the allowlist's Add sits in FormField's action slot, and the 1.65rem margin that lined it up is gone.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.list__add` guessed the label's height with a margin; the field row now stretches the button to the input, and a disabled Add's reason wrapper (a span) stretches and fills too.
- `fix(styles): the 47 fast transitions outside app.css name --ease-fast beside --duration-fast.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): they used the default ease while app.css's hovers used the pair; every sheet now agrees, and `source-guards.test.ts` fails a bare `var(--duration-fast)`.
- `fix(styles): every text colour that was --accent is --accent-text, and a source guard forbids the old spelling.` README 3.1, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): ten `color: var(--accent)` in accounts, apps, voice and the stage were 3.2:1 on the light theme; dark is unchanged. `source-guards.test.ts` scans every stylesheet.
- `fix(companion): the Speak button's accessible name contains the word it shows.` README 3.5, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): the button reads "Speak" and was named "Hold and say approve or decline", so a voice user saying "click Speak" found nothing; it is "Speak: hold and say approve or decline".
- `fix(halo): the caption lingers its 4 seconds after her reply, instead of fading with the edges in 400 ms.` README 3.1, ADR 0027 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): the idle phase set `opacity: 0` on the page and the caption is inside it, so the model's 4 s linger showed for 400 ms; the fade is now a `--live` factor on the edges' opacity, and the page stays opaque.
- `fix(companion): the composer answers a pointer, and its blocked state eases in and out.` README 3.1, ADR 0026 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist, state-coverage): the Talk textarea had no hover and no transition, so it snapped to the blocked wash when a turn began; it eases background and border on the fast duration and darkens its border under the pointer.
- `fix(companion): the Enable and Disable buttons are the same 32px as the other small outlined buttons.` README 3.1, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.aw-btn-toggle` was 30px beside the engine line's buttons and the icon buttons at 32px.
- `fix(companion): the welcome's engine line breaks a long reason instead of pushing the paper's edge out.` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep, state-coverage): the failed-start state prints the daemon's error verbatim, often a path with no space in it, and `.wl-engine` had no `overflow-wrap`.
- `fix(companion): a long parameter key on the decision card no longer runs over its value.` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep, visual-craft): `.sl-params dt` was a fixed 64px box that holds about seven uppercase characters, so a host tool's `recipient_email` overflowed onto the value; it grows to 45% of the row and then breaks inside the word.
- `fix(companion): a switched-off app is told to be switched back on under Apps in Main, not at a button that is gone.` README 3.5, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep, copy-auditor): the stopped-turn sentence said "Press Act here again in Main", a control no surface has any more; it names the Apps depth, where the on/off switch is.
- `fix(companion): each Enable or Disable button on Origins names its app, and its state is said once.` README 3.5, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): every row's button read just Disable or Enable and also carried `aria-pressed` beside a label that flips with the state; the name is "Disable <host>" now and `aria-pressed` is gone.
- `fix(chores): the hero's words hold two lines for the sentence and the hint that change under the pointer.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): the words column is centred in the hero and its line and hint changed height as marks were crossed, moving the title; `.ch-words .ch-line` holds 3em and `.ch-hint` 2.9em. `chores.css.test.ts` pins it.
- `fix(companion): the Record and Origins tabs are named regions, as Talk's conversation is.` README 3.5, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): both bodies were focusable divs with no role and no name; they are `role="region"` with `aria-label` Record and Origins.
- `fix(chores): the bench glance is announced by its label and its figures, not only "Open what she filed".` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): its `aria-label` replaced the button's text, so a screen reader lost the date, the money found and the visible label; the name is now "Bench, <date>: $X of $Y planted in its world. Open what she filed". `view.test.tsx` pins it.
- `fix(companion): the tape's finished line comes in the way its working line did, instead of swapping in one frame.` README 3.1, ADR 0026 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): each working step slid in on `line-in`, then the result replaced it bare; it carries the same class and a key now.
- `fix(voice): the scene sheet writes .vsc-wave and the narrow studio once each.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, code-optimizer): two rule blocks for `.vsc-wave` and two back-to-back `.vsc--studio` blocks in the 899px query are merged; a source guard fails on a top-level selector written twice.
- `fix(companion): the sheet loses the first-launch intro, the sim line and the fact card that nothing draws.` README 3.5, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep, code-optimizer): 55 lines of `companion.css` selected on `data-intro` (never set), `.wl-sim` and `.aw-fact` (no markup) or eased an opacity nothing changes; a guard now requires every `data-*` the sheet selects to be written by the view.
- `fix(chores): the on-the-edge flag is the chip's size, not a pill 2px shorter and 2px narrower.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.ch-edge-flag` was `2px 9px` beside `.ch-chip`'s `3px 10px` in the same rows; both are `3px 10px` now. `chores.css.test.ts` pins it.
- `fix(chores): a filter that leaves no chore says which kind and offers all of them back in one press.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, copy-auditor): "No chore here is of that kind." was a dead end; it now reads "No chore here is meant for home." (or work) with a "Show all N" button. `wired.test.tsx` pins it.
- `fix(voice): the listener take pads like the voice card beside it.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.vsc-take` padded 14px all round against `.vsc-voice`'s 14px 16px, so the nested cards' text started 2px apart across steps.
- `fix(companion): the seal answers a pointer, so her one button at rest no longer looks inert.` README 3.1, ADR 0026 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ux-reviewer): the seal had a focus ring and no hover while every other control in her window had one; the face scales to 1.08 on its existing transition, and a raised stamp or the hearing face keeps its own scale.
- `fix(voice): the hero waveform rises in with the head and the card instead of appearing under them.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): Pick and Hear mount `.vsc-hero` between two parts that rise 8px over 320ms and it popped; it now uses `vsc-in`, and Calm and reduced motion drop it with them.
- `fix(companion): the Approve and teal buttons darken their own token on hover, not a second hex.` README 3.1, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): the hovers were `#831343` and `#0c6178`, written beside bases that are tokens (`--paper-stamp`, the app's `--btn-primary`); they are a 15% mix to black of the base now, within 2 per channel of the old values.
- `fix(chores): the film part holds the shape of its still before the still or the film arrives.` README 3.5, ADR 0055 and 0087 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.ch-film__media` had a width and no height, so the still (0px) and the film (150px) jumped to full height when they loaded and pushed the text beneath; it is `aspect-ratio: 960 / 710` (the committed stills) with `object-fit: contain`. `chores.css.test.ts` pins it.
- `fix(voice): the rail's current-step bar and stage label ease as the studio moves on.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): the 2px bar and the stage label swapped to the accent in the frame the step changed; both ease colour on `--duration-fast`.
- `fix(companion): the Talk tab's count badge centres its figure at the default text size.` README 3.1, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.pip-n` is 18px high with a line of `calc(18px + step)`, 20px by default, so the figure sat 1px low; the line is the badge's height, as the seal's `.aw-badge` already has it.
- `fix(styles): the close confirm's Close and Keep buttons ease their hover wash.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): the one place a quit is decided was the last chrome control whose hover snapped; `background` now eases on `--duration-fast` / `--ease-fast`. Tests: styles/motion-tokens.test.
- `fix(chores): the depth's 14px gaps and pads are the density tokens, as the engine section's already are.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): the Chores sheet carried a 14px between the 12 and 16 of the density scale in its grids, tiles, glance, portals and quote; they read `--density-gap`, `--density-pad` and `--density-pad-sm` now. `chores.css.test.ts` forbids a 14px in a padding, gap or margin.
- `fix(voice): a take, the voice card, the wave frame and its bars ease the state that recolours them.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): picking a listener, playing her voice and starting a mic take swapped borders, a ring and bar colours in one frame; they now ease on `--duration-fast` and the picked ring fades by opacity.
- `fix(companion): the text buttons (done, Main is hidden) answer a pointer and ease it.` README 3.1, ADR 0026 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.aw-textbtn` had no hover, the only pointer target in the ledger without one; the link darkens to the paper ink on the fast duration.
- `fix(components): the push-to-talk pill is named by the word it shows, not by its tooltip sentence.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): `aria-label` copied the title, so a screen reader heard "Release to send" for a pill that reads "Listening" (a label that does not contain the visible text); the name now comes from the content and the title stays the tooltip and description. Tests: components/PushToTalk.test.
- `fix(companion): answering a card dims its buttons on the fast duration instead of jumping to 0.45.` README 3.1, ADR 0026 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist, state-coverage): `.aw-btn` eased background, transform and colour but not opacity, so Approve, Decline, Speak and Send snapped to 0.45 the moment they were disabled.
- `fix(chores): the line that says what a press did is reserved, so its words no longer push the page down.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): the copy / hand-over note under a chore's actions and the "Opened ..." notes under the portals and the sources were zero-high until they spoke, then wrapped the row or grew the panel by a line; `.ch-said` holds the line (and the whole row in the actions). `chores.css.test.ts` pins it.
- `fix(voice): the engine option and the rail's steps ease their hover, and the chosen ring fades in.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.vsc-opt` and `button.vsc-step` swapped background with no transition and the chosen ring popped as a box-shadow; they use `--duration-fast` like `.vsc-place`, and the ring is an opacity layer.
- `fix(styles): the window buttons, the push-to-talk pill and the tab chips ease their hovers, and the pill's dot takes the palette's red and rests under Calm.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): four chrome controls changed colour with no transition; the listening and error dots were a literal `#e81123` outside the close button's fixed-red exception and now use `--status-error`; `ptt-pulse` ran forever under Calm and now stops there. Tests: styles/motion-tokens.test.
- `fix(companion): the ledger's selected tab marks its edge by a bar that fades in, not a box-shadow that snaps.` README 3.1, ADR 0026 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): switching Talk, Record and Origins eased the background but the 2px edge was an inset box-shadow that jumped; it is a `::before` bar on opacity, and the label colour eases with the background.
- `fix(chores): choosing another part of a chore fades the panel in on the depth clock instead of swapping it in one frame.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.ch-panel` is remounted per part with no entrance; it now runs an opacity-only keyframe on `--dur-depth` (0 ms under Calm). `chores.css.test.ts` pins it.
- `fix(voice): the field holds two lines for the sentence that changes under the pointer, so the places stay put.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): hovering a place swapped a one-line standing for a two-line hint and the places slid down about 26px; `.vsc--field .vsc-lead` now holds 3.1em like the Mind field.
- `fix(styles): the shared controls ease every state they change: the press, the disabled fade, the input edge and the row wash, and a primary button answers the pointer.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.btn:active` scaled with no transform in its transition and `:disabled` snapped its opacity; `.input` and `.table td` had no transition or hover; `.btn--primary` had no hover at all (it gives 12% to the foreground). All on `--duration-fast` / `--ease-fast`, transform and opacity beyond the colour pair only; an invalid input keeps its error edge under the pointer. Tests: styles/motion-tokens.test.
- `fix(voice): the Space chip at the end of Hold to talk no longer pads the button's right edge deeper than its left.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): the chip's 6px leading margin sat on the trailing edge, 16px of inset against 10px; `:last-child` clears it.
- `fix(chores): a turn dot of the replay answers the pointer instead of sitting dead under it.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ux-reviewer): `.ch-dot` had `cursor: pointer` and no hover; it now takes the hover wash and full opacity, eased on `--duration-fast`, like the ledger rows beside it.
- `fix(companion): the pin and put-away buttons ease their hover and pressed state instead of snapping.` README 3.1, ADR 0026 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.ico-btn` had a hover background and a pressed border and no transition, next to `.aw-btn` in the same header which eased; it now eases background, colour and border on the fast duration.
- `fix(components): FormField takes an action that sits level with its control, so a button beside a field needs no magic margin.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): the accounts list's Add button was lined up with `margin-top: 1.65rem`, a guess at the label's height that breaks at the other type step; `action` renders the control and the button in one stretched grid row above the hint. The accounts scene's adoption is its owner's. Tests: components/FormField.test, styles/motion-tokens.test.
- `fix(chores): the bench glance, the facet list, the replay dots and ledger rows and the map mark ease their hover instead of jumping.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): only 2 of the 6 pressable rules of `chores.css` had a transition; the glance, the facets (and the current-part swap), the dots and the ledger rows now ease border, background or opacity on `--duration-fast`, and the map mark eases its fill with its lift. `chores.css.test.ts` pins it.
- `fix(voice): the install bar fills by scale on a transform, and stills under Calm.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.vs-progress > span` animated `width` on every poll and ran its fill and pulse under Calm; it now scales a full-width fill and the module's sheet carries the Calm and reduced-motion rules.
- `fix(companion): the plan's eyebrow is read at the label tier, not 11px at the compact text size.` README 3.5, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): `.lg-plan__eyebrow` was `calc(11px + var(--type-step))`, 11px with Text size compact, under the sheet's own 12px floor; it is `var(--t-label)` now, and a guard reads every font size in the sheet.
- `fix(styles): --duration-fast gets an easing pair, --ease-fast, and the button, input and row hovers spend it.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): the three hover transitions in the sheet named a duration and fell back to the browser's default ease (slow start); they now ease on `cubic-bezier(0.2, 0, 0, 1)`. Other stylesheets that spend `--duration-fast` can adopt the pair. Tests: styles/motion-tokens.test.
- `fix(halo): the caption's who-label is a spelled 14px, not a step from a sheet the halo page never loads.` README 3.1, ADR 0027 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `calc(12px + var(--type-step))` read a property only `styles/app.css` defines, so on this page it was invalid and the label fell to the inherited 16px; it is now 14px (the 12px label tier plus the comfortable step), pinned by a guard that every custom property the sheet reads it also defines.
- `fix(voice): a key chip on a primary button is set in the button's own ink, not white on cyan.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): `.btn--primary .vs-kbd` used `--on-primary` (#fff) on the dark theme's #06b6d4 button, about 2.4:1 at 12px; it now mixes `--primary-fg` like the label beside it.
- `fix(styles): a text-safe accent token, --accent-text, for the light theme where --accent is 3.2:1.` README 3.1, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): `--accent` (#0891b2) is 3.17:1 on the canvas and 2.97:1 on the recessed rung, fine for a line or a fill and not for a word; `--accent-text` is #0b6a85 (4.96:1 at the weakest) in light and `var(--accent)` in dark, unchanged. Adoption by the text uses in stage, chores, apps, accounts and voice is the owners' to land. Tests: styles/motion-tokens.test.
- `fix(chores): a chore card's lift and focus ring are no longer cut off by its content-visibility box.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.ch-grid__item` paints inside its own box, so the tile's 2px hover lift and its focus ring (2px line, 2px offset) were clipped at the card edge; the item holds 4px of padding and returns it as margin, so nothing moves. `chores.css.test.ts` pins it.
- `fix(voice): the module's stylesheet loses the rules of the studio it no longer draws, and the tile and orb nothing mounts.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, code-optimizer): about half of `modules/voice/voice.css` (the frame, rail, dock, presence, takes, style lines, `vs-rise`, four container queries) styled classes no component writes since the cutover, and `VoiceTile` and `Orb` had no caller; a source guard now fails on a `vs-` class nothing draws.
- `fix(styles): the global reduced-motion rule plays every animation once, so an infinite loop is still, not a flicker.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): `animation-duration: 1ms !important` alone made the bell, the shafts and every scene loop repeat 1000 times a second; the rule now also sets `animation-iteration-count: 1` and covers `::before`/`::after`. Tests: styles/motion-tokens.test.
- `fix(chores): the small accent-coloured words of the Chores depth are read at 4.5:1 in the light theme.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): the eyebrow, the "Only Athena" zone label, the facet group titles, the source links and the replay's speaker labels were 13-14px `var(--accent)` (#0891b2, about 3.2:1 on the light ground); they are `var(--primary)` (4.6:1) now, as the surface, first act and Mind eyebrows already are. `chores.css.test.ts` forbids `color: var(--accent)`.
- `fix(setup): the engine fact's busy label ends in the single ellipsis character like the other busy labels.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, copy-auditor): "checking the engine..." used three dots while every other busy label uses one character. Tests: modules/setup/readiness.test.
- `fix(scenes): the first act's address field and the Mind depth's brain path ease their hover like the surface's ask field.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): two of three twin text fields had no hover and no transition; all three now ease border-color on `--duration-fast`. Tests: scenes/{waking,mind}/*.css.test.
- `fix(mind): the field holds two lines for the sentence that changes under the pointer, so the tiles stay put.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.mind__line` reserved one line while the surface's twin reserves two; at the 900 px window the Look sentence wraps on hover and slid the grid down 25 px; the field now holds 3em. Tests: scenes/mind/mind.css.test.
- `fix(mind): the Look tile and its eyebrow say "system theme", not a bare "system".` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, copy-auditor): the Look badge and the level's eyebrow read SYSTEM, LIGHT or DARK with nothing saying what the word was about; they now read "system theme", "light theme", "dark theme". Tests: scenes/mind/view.test.
- `fix(mind): an instrument's badge carries the hue of its standing, so "needs you" no longer looks like "set".` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, visual-craft): every badge on the field was a neutral dot (which StatusDot defines as no state at all); Engine, Brain and Microphone now draw success, warning, pending or neutral from the Setup module's own standing. Tests: scenes/mind/view.test.
- `fix(desktop): the voice install ring slides to each poll's progress instead of stepping.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `VoiceArt`'s ring set `stroke-dasharray` from each poll's answer and the arc jumped by whole percentage steps; it now eases over `--dur-settle` / `--ease-settle` (the browser interpolates the one attribute between answers, no script per frame) and holds still under Calm. Tests: art/glyphs.test.tsx.
- `fix(surface): a light that needs the person says so in its name, not only in a dot.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): attention was a generated bullet in the warning colour and nothing a screen reader hears; the light is now named "Accounts, 1 of 3 connected, needs you". Tests: scenes/surface/view.test.
- `fix(desktop): connecting an account inks its drawing over the depth clock instead of snapping from outline to colour.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `AccountArt` flipped its strokes and washes between the muted and accent tokens in one frame, and the plinth and plug dot switched between `none` and a colour, which cannot interpolate; every fill is now a `color-mix` in both states and the shapes ease stroke and fill on `--dur-depth` (0 ms under Calm). Tests: art/glyphs.test.tsx.
- `fix(surface): the ask row says it is handing the words over, and a refusal reads as an error.` README 3.1, ADR 0026, 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, state-coverage): while the hand-over was pending the button only went disabled and the note kept the idle sentence, and a refusal was in the same grey as the hint; sending now says so on the button and in the status, and a problem takes the error colour. Tests: scenes/surface/interact.test (new), surface.css.test.
- `fix(desktop): a tendril of the bell lights up by fading, not by snapping its weight.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): switching an app or account on jumped the tendril from a 1.4 px, 30% stroke to 2.8 px, 100% (stroke-width cannot animate under ADR 0089) and popped its tip dot; the lit weight is now a second stroke over the resting one that fades on opacity, the dot fades with it, and the lean and both fades use `--dur-settle` instead of a literal 600 ms. Tests: bell/Bell.test.tsx.
- `fix(surface): the summon button, the ask field and Hand it to her answer a pointer and ease on the fast duration.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.surface__summon` and `.surface__send` had a pointer cursor and no hover, no transition, and the field no hover; all three now ease background, border-color and opacity on `--duration-fast`. Tests: scenes/surface/surface.css.test.
- `fix(stage): Rise and the breadcrumb fade in on the dive's clock instead of popping into the bar.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): both are mounted by the dive that needs them and appeared fully drawn on frame 1; they now play an opacity-only keyframe on `--dur-depth`.
- `fix(desktop): the engine section's gaps and pads are the density tokens, not 10, 12 and 14 px one-offs.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): house style rule 4 says density is a variable; five literal gaps and two pads (`14px 16px`, `10px 14px`, gaps of 10 and 12) now read `--density-pad-sm` / `--density-pad`, so the notice agrees with `ProblemNote` and the cards trade 2 px of height each. Tests: scenes/shared/EngineSection.test.tsx.
- `fix(waking): the forward button names the next step instead of a clipped question.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, copy-auditor): "Next: do you want to talk to her" was a question lower-cased with its mark cut off; it is "Next: Reach" and "Next: Voice", the names the pills carry. Tests: scenes/waking/view.test.
- `fix(console): the console's frame sheet loses the bridged-module wrapper rule nothing draws any more.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, code-optimizer): `.console__bridge` had zero consumers since the cutover; a test now pins that the sheet styles only classes root.tsx draws.
- `fix(waking): the steps hang from the top, so swapping to a shorter step no longer slides the pills.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.waking` centred both columns, so the pills and the title moved by half the height difference between the engine step and the voice step on every Next; it is `align-items: start` now with the Mind level's 4vh inset. Tests: scenes/waking/waking.css.test.
- `fix(desktop): the engine restart notice fades in on the depth clock instead of popping into the column.` README 3.5, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.engines__notice` mounted with no entrance while its neighbours stayed put; it now runs an opacity-only `--dur-depth` / `--ease-depth` fade, which is 0 ms under Calm. Tests: scenes/shared/EngineSection.test.tsx.
- `fix(stage): the dock rises into place on the dive's clock instead of standing there on the first frame.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): the footer is mounted at depth 1 and was fully drawn at once beside a scene that eased in over 360 ms; it now plays an opacity and transform keyframe on `--dur-depth`.
- `fix(accounts): the connect rail's steps ease as they pass, and a rule that styled nothing is gone.` README 3.5, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): next, now and done swapped colour, border and fill with no transition while the cards beside them ease; the step and its number now ease colour, border-color and background over --duration-fast, and `.stages__step + .stages__step::before { content: none }` (no ::before exists to cancel) is removed. Tests: scenes/accounts/view.test.
- `fix(waking): a done step shows a check and is named done, instead of turning green behind its number.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): the step pill told done by a green fill alone and its name read "1Mind"; a done pill now shows a check mark, is named "Mind, done", and the digit is hidden from the name. Tests: scenes/waking/view.test.
- `fix(desktop): choosing the other engine moves the card emphasis across in 150 ms instead of jumping.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.engines__card[data-chosen]` swapped border and a 24px glow shadow with no transition; the ring and glow now sit on an `::after` layer that fades on opacity, the border colour eases, both on `--duration-fast` like the other scenes' cards, and Calm holds them still. Tests: scenes/shared/EngineSection.test.tsx.
- `fix(stage): the gauge's ticks light on the mark's own clock and no longer jump while it slides.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): ticks swapped background and opacity instantly while the mark slid for 360 ms; they now light by the opacity of an accent layer on `--dur-depth`.
- `fix(waking): the step pills and the Later link ease their hover, and the pills answer a pointer at all.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.waking__step` had no hover and no transition, the current and done states swapped in one frame; all three pointer controls now ease background, border-color and color on `--duration-fast`. Tests: scenes/waking/waking.css.test.
- `fix(accounts): pressing Disconnect no longer drops the keyboard's place.` README 3.5, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): the pressed button was replaced by the question, so focus fell to the document; Keep it now takes it, and Disconnect takes it back when Keep it is pressed (the same fix as Apps' Forget). Tests: scenes/accounts/interact.test.
- `fix(desktop): the engine section's busy label says Checking… with the ellipsis the other busy labels use.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, copy-auditor): the Check again button read "Checking..." while the companion's twin and the accounts scene use the single character. Tests: scenes/shared/EngineSection.test.tsx, scenes/waking/view.test.tsx.
- `fix(apps): pressing Forget no longer drops the keyboard's place.` README 3.5, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): the pressed button was replaced by the question, so focus fell to the document; Keep now takes it, and Forget this app takes it back when Keep is pressed. Tests: scenes/apps/interact.test.
- `fix(stage): the light shafts stand still on a machine that asks for less motion, even with Motion set to Full.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, accessibility-checker): the sheet's global 1 ms reduced-motion duration turned four infinite sways into a flicker; the shafts now carry `animation: none` under the media query too.
- `fix(desktop): the engine summary line carries its standing on a dot, not in the colour of its words.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): `.engines__fact` turned the whole sentence success/warning green or amber at 14px; it now shows the setup module's own `STANDING_TONE` as a `StatusDot` and keeps the words muted. Tests: scenes/shared/EngineSection.test.tsx.
- `fix(scenes): the eyebrow of the surface, the first act and the Mind depth is read at 4.5:1 in the light theme.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): the three eyebrows (13px, uppercase) were `--accent`, #0891b2 on #eceef2, about 3.2:1; they are `--primary` now, which the palette pins at 4.6:1. Tests: scenes/{surface,waking,mind}/*.css.test.
- `fix(apps): the page chips and tool pills answer a pointer, and the focused page is not told by a border colour alone.` README 3.5, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist, accessibility-checker): the chip's close and label had no hover, the tool pill and the chip's focused border changed with no ease, and the focused chip differed from the rest by border colour only; they now ease background, border-color or color over --duration-fast, the focused chip takes the wash and aria-current. Tests: scenes/apps/apps.css.test, scenes/apps/view.test.
- `fix(stage): naming a depth in the dock no longer widens its pill by 2 px and shoves the roots beside it.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): the current root alone carried a 1 px border, so the row jumped on every move; every root now holds a transparent one.
- `fix(apps): hovering an app no longer pushes the orbs down.` README 3.5, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): the header line reserved one line while an app's summary takes two, so the orb under the pointer slid away as the summary arrived; it now reserves two (min-height 1.5em to 3em, as Accounts already does). Tests: scenes/apps/apps.css.test.
- `fix(desktop): an engine's standing is the shared badge, so its hue rides a dot and not the words.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): the engine cards hand-rolled a pill with success/warning colour on 13px text (the pair house style 4b says keeps measuring under AA) and a 1px 10px pad off the app's badge; they now use `Badge` with a tone. Tests: scenes/shared/EngineSection.test.tsx.
- `fix(stage): the bar's and the dock's buttons ease their hover on the fast duration, and the dock's Athena button answers a hover at all.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep, ui-perfectionist): stage.css gave Rise, the crumbs, the depth roots and the dock button no transition while every scene button has one; guarded by stage/stage.css.test.ts.
- `chore(tooling): adopt the scan-sweep skill with a ui-perfectionist lens and a context map of the desktop UI.` AGENTS.md rules: .claude/skills/scan-sweep is the registry skill, .claude/scan-sweep holds the overlay (gates, token and motion rules) and an 18-context map of the UI built by make-map.mjs; ui-perfectionist is the local lens for spacing, transitions and flow. Tests: none (tooling); verified by `coverage.mjs --next`.
- `fix(desktop): the depths fit the smallest window, and the stage says where you are once.` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0087 and 0089 (redesign phase 10): the narrow layouts began at 900 px, which is the shell's minimum width, so the smallest window got the stacked layout and the surface's five lights fell below the fold; they now begin under 900, and every scene was read at 900x600 (about a 1080p screen at 150%); the scenes are no longer a live region (a whole scene read on every move is noise), and a visually hidden status says the place once (Athena, Apps, an app). Tests: stage/Stage.test.tsx.
- `feat(desktop): the cutover: the console is Main, the bar and the old views are gone, and the first paint is a budget the build pins.` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0087, 0088, 0089, 0090, 0095 (redesign phase 9b): deleted the module bar, the app root, the registry, the flag, the five modules' views and stylesheets, the bridge, the Rust `browser` alias and `layout_module`, and the components only the old views used; the Rust selection is a shape (`page` or `scene`, starting as the scene) and `layout:changed` carries the shape alone; each depth is a `live.tsx` (hook, crumbs, band; its view a lazy chunk) plus an `index.tsx` with fixtures for the harness and tests; the shipped playbooks load on first ask (`stores/playbooks.ts`) instead of 2.7 MB of JSON in the first frame, and the surface and Chores say counting or loading until they arrive; first-paint JS is about 125 kB gzipped, pinned by console/graph.test; the dock is a row under every depth, and the light theme's depth tones cool; the preview harness is scene-first. Tests: console/graph.test, console/scenes.test (registry and contract), stores/playbooks.test, hook and surface cases, motion-tokens (light), layout.rs. Rust 112 tests, clippy clean; 1,125 vitest tests; `pnpm perf:all` green.
- `feat(desktop): the Mind depth: five drawn instruments (engine, brain, look, data, microphone), a level for each, and Calm as a choice in Look.` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0029, 0085, 0087, 0089, 0094 (redesign phase 9a): scenes/mind is the Setup module's own view-model drawn as a field and levels, so the facts, the engine's remedy, the failure to start with Try again, the restart notice, the theme and text size with their previews, the brain path as a draft committed on Enter, the microphone's one ask and the store path are the module's, verbatim; Calm (system, calm, full) is new in Look through `setMotion`; Page and Voice link to the depths that own them; "Show the welcome again" is a flag, not a row; EngineSection is shared by the first act and the engine level; sentence helpers and ThemePreview are exported from the module's view. `pnpm perf:mind` 0 idle frame requests. Tests: scenes/mind/{view,interact}.test, console/scenes.test.
- `feat(desktop): the Apps depth: a field of windows of light, one app composed on its own, a read-only tool level and the page's band, with the depth following Rust and the tabs.` README 3.4 and 3.5, ADR 0029, 0063, 0087, 0088, 0091 (redesign phase 6): scenes/apps is the Browser module's behaviour as three levels (the field with registered apps drawn as windows whose standing is a shape and a word, register, open pages; an app with its switch, Open and two-press Forget; a tool as the page said it with the person's pin and no class); useBrowserModel is shared with the module and takes `onShown` so the scene moves to a page only once its tab exists; StripView (the module's strip) becomes the band above a page; console/follow.ts follows a page Rust selects, a changed focus and a last tab closed (a tab appearing in a scene is not followed); layout:changed reaches the console with its shape; the gate picks a free port so two runs never share a server. `pnpm perf:apps` 0 idle frame requests. Tests: scenes/apps/{model,view,interact}.test, console/follow.test.
- `feat(desktop): Accounts is a depth: a field of accounts with a drawing each, one account's own staged scene, and an allow-list editor, over the Connectors module's own model.` README 3.1, 3.5 and 4, ADR 0021, 0062, 0087, 0090 (redesign phase 8, Accounts): scenes/accounts draws the field (the hovered account's name is display type at the centre, standing by shape and word), `accounts/<id>` (connect as three stages, the seal sentence, probe age, Check now, on and off, the writes switch and its hint, reads and GATED writes) and `accounts/<id>/allowlist` (one entry per row, a draft until Add, Escape drops it); the records notice stays an undismissable alert, Disconnect still takes two presses, and every safety sentence is verbatim; `useConnectorsModel` is shared by the module and the scene, and `ConnectorRow.probedAge` is new. `pnpm perf:accounts` 0 idle frame requests. Preview: preview.html?scene=accounts&fixture=typical&at=%23/accounts/notion. Tests: scenes/accounts/{view,interact}.test, modules/connectors/model.test.
- `feat(desktop): the Chores depth: a field with the map as its hero, a composed scene and a drawing for every chore, and the replay of a run as a place of its own.` README 3.5, ADR 0040, 0053, 0055, 0087, 0089, 0090, 0093 (redesign phase 7): scenes/chores draws `chores` (the map, totals, filter and sort, a card and a verdict per chore, the simulated line), `chores/<chore>` (art/chore.tsx draws each chore from its own portals, gates, traps and value, labelled stylised; every part of the old Layer one click away; Copy, Hand it to Athena, Open) and `chores/<chore>/run/<turn>` (play, Back, Next, arrows, the turn rail, the cards filed on each turn, show all); `usePlaybooksModel({ afterOpen })` is shared with the module, so the console makes the Browser tab and does not select the Browser (Apps has not landed, so the scene stays and says so). `pnpm perf:chores` green, 0 idle frame requests. Preview: preview.html?scene=chores. Tests: scenes/chores/{model,view,wired}.test, art/chore.test, modules/playbooks/hook.test.
- `refactor(desktop): depth scenes share one contract and a registry, and docs/redesign/porting-a-depth.md says how to port one.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 and 0090 (redesign phases 6 to 8, groundwork): scenes/types.ts is `DepthScene` (a Live and a preview that take `{ path, go }`, crumb labellers, an optional page-shape Band); console/scenes.tsx holds `DEPTH_SCENES` (empty until a port lands) and draws any other root through the bridge; the console gathers every scene's labels and the Apps band; the scene preview runs the stage's own reducer at `?at=` so a scene's links dive. Tests: console/scenes.test.tsx.
- `feat(desktop): the Voice depth: a field of two places with the daemon's standing, the studio drawn as an act with its keys, and settings as two instruments.` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0028, 0087, 0089 and 0090 (redesign phase 8, Voice): scenes/voice has the field (Studio and Settings, her bell, the plain gate sentences), `voice/studio` (a clickable rail with Restart, a stylised drawing per engine, the step under attention in display type, the hero waveform of her real voice while she speaks and of the microphone's level, labelled stylised, while a take records, Whisper and OpenAI side by side, and the Enter, B, S, 1, 2 and held Space keys on the document with the dock chips) and `voice/settings` (Output and Input, Refresh, Reopen studio, the hotkey hint, Whisper's models, the OpenAI key that is never shown back with Replace, Remove and Keep the stored key, the test take); `useVoiceModel` is shared by the module and the scene, a finished studio is reopened on arrival and finishing lands in settings; the meters are the module's own and no frame is requested unless a take records or her voice plays. `pnpm perf:voice` 0 idle frame requests, 1 loop. Preview: preview.html?scene=voice&fixture=typical&at=%23/voice/studio. Tests: scenes/voice/{view,keys,interact}.test.- `refactor(desktop): depth scenes share one contract and a registry, and docs/redesign/porting-a-depth.md says how to port one.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 and 0090 (redesign phases 6 to 8, groundwork): scenes/types.ts is `DepthScene` (a Live and a preview that take `{ path, go }`, crumb labellers, an optional page-shape Band); console/scenes.tsx holds `DEPTH_SCENES` (empty until a port lands) and draws any other root through the bridge; the console gathers every scene's labels and the Apps band; the scene preview runs the stage's own reducer at `?at=` so a scene's links dive. Tests: console/scenes.test.tsx.
- `feat(desktop): the first act and the surface: setup as the opening of the scene, Athena and five lights, and a console that mounts the stage on the real stores behind an opt-in flag.` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0026, 0085, 0087, 0090 (redesign phase 5): scenes/waking is the letter's rules (engine fact and remedy, first app, microphone, gated Wake her) as three steps that light the bell; scenes/surface is her pose from the mirror, a tendril per app and account, an ask box that hands the sentence to her own window (decision D1) and five lights with live stats; console/ wires the stage to depth, athena and settings and bridges every depth not yet ported to its module's existing view so nothing is unreachable; `pnpm tauri:console` or athena.ui=console turns it on, the bar stays the default; useSetupModel is shared by the module and the scenes, and WindowControls is extracted from app.tsx (its close-confirm Escape now marks the key handled). `pnpm perf:surface` 0 idle frame requests. Preview: preview.html?scene=surface|waking|console. Tests: scenes/surface/{model,view}.test, scenes/waking/view.test, console/scenes.test, lib/ui.test.
- `feat(desktop): Rust knows the window's two shapes, page and scene, and the stage tells it which one it is in.` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0088 (redesign phase 4): layout.rs adds PAGE_SHAPE and SCENE_SHAPE, `Selection::is_page` (true for `page` and for the old `browser` id until the module bar is deleted) used by apply and by capture.rs, and `layout:changed` now carries the shape; depth/shape.ts sends `layout_select` with the shape's id when it changes (once at the start, never repeated, a refusal logged not thrown) and a test reads layout.rs to keep the TypeScript and Rust spellings equal; the stage gains a `band` slot, drawn only in the page shape under the 36 px bar and 40 px tall, the 76 px the chrome keeps. Tests: layout.rs the_page_shape_shows_a_page_and_the_scene_shape_does_not and the_two_shape_ids_are_distinct_from_each_other_and_from_a_module, depth/shape.test.ts, stage/Stage.test.tsx. Rust 112 tests pass, clippy clean.
- `feat(desktop): Athena as a bell of light with a tendril per thing she may touch, posed from the mirror and animated without a frame loop.` README 3.1, ADR 0026, 0087, 0089 (redesign phase 3): src/bell/pose.ts derives resting, working, hearing or asking from exactly what `athena:status` reports (speaking is left out until the mirror carries it); Bell.tsx is a pure SVG whose pulse, two-joint sway, ripples and lean are CSS on transform, rotate and opacity (the compositor's, so a settled window requests no frames), with per-instance gradient ids so two bells keep their own colours; the stage's dock wears it. `pnpm perf:bell`: 0 frame loops, 0 idle frame requests, frame p95 16.8 ms. The companion's stamped seal keeps its own drawing (its sizes are a closed table pinned against companion.rs); reuse is a polish-phase question. Preview: preview.html?scene=bell. Tests: bell/pose.test.ts, bell/Bell.test.tsx (including no script per frame, transform and opacity only, Calm off), stage/Stage.test.tsx.
- `feat(desktop): the stage: the water, the gauge, the breadcrumb, Rise, the dock and the depth transition, previewed over placeholder scenes and held to the perf gate.` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0087, 0088, 0089 (redesign phase 2): src/stage/ draws a move as two layers (pure state machine in layers.ts: interrupted moves keep at most two ghosts, calm is one layer, a stuck move is flushed) animated on transform and opacity only; the backdrop stacks four flat tones cross-faded by opacity, soft CSS light shafts, and a snow canvas that drifts only during a move on the shared clock and then stops asking for frames; the page shape draws just the band; Escape rises and leaves a text field its own; depth/store.ts mirrors the hash with replaceState; `pnpm perf:stage` builds, serves and drives dive, dive, rise, rise and an interrupted dive: frame p95 16.8 ms, 0 long tasks, 1 loop, 0 idle frame requests. Preview: preview.html?scene=stage. Tests: stage/layers.test.ts, stage/Stage.test.tsx, depth/store.test.ts.
- `feat(desktop): the depth model: a fixed path grammar, an interruptible reducer, a hash mirror, crumbs and the window's two shapes, as pure code.` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0087 and 0088 amended (redesign phase 1): src/depth/model.ts has five roots with the user's words (Apps, Chores, Accounts, Voice, Mind) mapped to the unchanged module ids, a grammar that makes every place at most three moves from the surface, total `reduce` (an invalid or repeated destination is ignored, a rise at the surface stays there, a stale settle cannot cancel a newer move), `parseHash` that falls back to the surface and never throws, and `shapeOf` (page only at apps/<app>/page). Nothing uses it yet. Tests: depth/model.test.ts (every legal path round-trips, rises, and obeys the depth bound).
- `feat(desktop): a performance gate, one shared animation clock, a Calm motion setting and the depth and motion tokens, before any scene exists.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (redesign phase 0): `pnpm perf` drives a page in Edge (or Chrome) and judges frame p95, dropped frames, long tasks, concurrent frame loops and idle frame requests against budgets, and its self-test proves it passes a still page and fails a janky one; lib/motion.ts is the single rAF loop (idle when nothing subscribes, paused when hidden) plus the system/calm/full choice written as data-motion, stored in settings/motion; app.css gains --dur-depth, --ease-depth and --depth-0..3 as mixes of the palette. Tests: lib/motion.test.ts, scripts/perf-budgets.test.ts, stores/settings.test.ts, styles/motion-tokens.test.ts.
- `docs(redesign): the desktop app's redesign as one scene with depths (Fathom) is planned, with a parity matrix, three ADRs and the contest winner kept as reference.` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0087 (one scene with depths), 0088 (two layout shapes around the native page webview), 0089 (motion budgets and a perf gate): docs/redesign/fathom.md (phases 0 to 10, budgets, risks, owner decisions), docs/redesign/parity.md (every capability with its file and a proof column), docs/redesign/reference/fathom (prior art, never pasted). Tests: none, no code changed.
- `fix(setup): a status that lands while an engine restart is queued no longer makes Setup or the welcome say ready on the engine being torn down.` README 3.5, ADR 0085 amended (first-run-setup full r2 robustness-1, -2): while a restart is queued or in flight the daemon store holds only the latest event for the engine in flight and re-reads the daemon's status when the queue drains; an event naming another engine than the one last asked for is ignored. Tests: stores/first-run-engine-late-status.test.ts.
- `fix(setup): when Athena cannot start on the chosen engine, the welcome and Setup say why and offer Try again.` README 3.5, ADR 0085 amended (first-run-setup full r2 value-failed-start-dead-end): choosing the engine again restarts a daemon that failed or stopped on it; the welcome shows the failure reason with Try again (and Use the other engine when one is found) instead of Check again in a moment; Setup shows the reason and Try again on the engine instead of the could-not-check-engines line. Tests: companion/model.test.ts, companion/views.test.tsx, modules/setup/model.test.ts, modules/setup/view.test.tsx.
- `fix(setup): a second engine choice while a restart is under way waits for it, a failed restart reads as could not start, and a choice before the daemon has reported always restarts.` README 3.5, ADR 0085 amended (first-run-setup full r1 robustness-1, -2, -4): useDaemon.restart marks starting on the target engine at once and failed (with the error text) on rejection; restarts run one at a time and chooseFirstRunEngine compares again when its turn comes; an unloaded store is never compared with its placeholder engine. Tests: stores/first-run-engine-inflight.test.ts.
- `fix(setup): a restart that cannot start the new engine now says so, instead of leaving the old engine's status standing.` README 3.5, ADR 0085 amended (first-run-setup full r1 robustness-2, Rust half): daemon_restart and the launch both set the status to failed, with the message, no url and the engine that was attempted, through one helper (mark_start_failed) before returning the error. Test: daemon.rs a_failed_start_says_failed_on_the_attempted_engine_with_no_url.
- `test(engines): KNOWN_ENGINES in daemon.rs must list exactly ENGINE_IDS, in order.` README 3.5, ADR 0085 amended (first-run-setup lite r2 robustness-2): lib/engines.test.ts reads src-tauri/src/daemon.rs as text and fails when the list is not found or differs; Python's ENGINES also holds the API engine and is left out. Test: lib/engines.test.ts.
- `test(setup): Setup's engine branch is one exported function, and the first-run choice is pinned for a restart that rejects.` README 3.5, ADR 0085 (first-run-setup lite r2 robustness-1, TypeScript half): `chooseSetupEngine` in stores/settings.ts is the onboarded ? setEngine : chooseFirstRunEngine branch moved unchanged out of setup/index.ts; a restart that rejects after the row was written rejects with that error and leaves the row; the Rust launch read stays untested. Tests: stores/first-run-engine.test.ts.
- `fix(setup): neither Setup nor the welcome reads the daemon store's placeholder engine as the running one before the daemon has reported.` README 3.5, ADR 0085 amended (first-run-setup lite r2 value-3, craft-4): useDaemon's `loaded` goes through SetupSources (`daemonLoaded`, default true) and the welcome inputs; while it is false engineFact is unknown (Athena has not yet said which engine she is on) and engineView reads as looking; EMPTY_DAEMON, the reducer and chooseFirstRunEngine are unchanged, and a not-loaded daemon still restarts on a differing choice (pinned). Tests: readiness.test.ts, companion/model.test.ts, stores/first-run-engine.test.ts.
- `fix(setup): a daemon on the chosen engine that failed or stopped no longer reads as still starting up, in Setup or on the welcome.` README 3.5, ADR 0085 amended (first-run-setup lite r2 value-2): engineFact and voiceSentence keep 'still starting' for starting only, failed says Athena could not start (standing missing) and stopped says she is not running; engineView takes the daemon health as a new optional last parameter, passed from live.tsx, and says ready only for ready (starting and stopped read as looking, failed as missing); a caller that omits it is unchanged. Tests: readiness.test.ts, companion/model.test.ts.
- `fix(setup): the app launches on the stored engine, a first-run engine choice moves the daemon at once, and the engine fact is never ready while the daemon runs another.` README 3.5, ADR 0085 (first-run-setup lite r1 value-1): daemon.rs launch_engine reads settings/engine (known ids only, default otherwise); chooseFirstRunEngine writes the row then restarts when the daemon's engine differs, used by Setup while not onboarded and by the welcome; engineFact and the welcome's engineView need the running engine to match. Settings after onboarding is unchanged. Tests: daemon.rs launch_engine, readiness.test.ts, stores/first-run-engine.test.ts, companion/model.test.ts.
- `fix(brain): a fact with an empty key, or a key holding any line break a reader splits on, is refused before its card and by the writer.` README 2 invariant 1, ADR 0082 amended (memory full r2 craft-7 follow-up): Brain.write_fact refuses a key empty after strip() and Brain.write_procedural a trigger empty after strip(), before anything is minted or written; frontmatter._LINE_BREAKS and validators.FACT_KEY_LINE_BREAKS are the ten characters str.splitlines() breaks on (LF, CR, VT, FF, U+001C to U+001E, NEL, LS, PS), and fact_text() refuses an empty key first; a legal value's bytes are unchanged, and a key with a double quote is pinned as round-tripping. Tests: tests/core/test_brain_frontmatter.py, tests/core/test_validators.py, tests/core/test_brain_store.py, tests/core/test_catalog.py.
- `fix(ledger): the rollup counts calls, not turns, and says how many rows it could not price.` README 1 act 4, ADR 0083 amended: GET /ledger/rollup items rename `turns` to `calls` (every row in the key, speech requests included) and add `unpriced` (rows whose cost_usd is NULL, so cost_usd is a floor while it is above 0); nothing under apps/ reads the route. Tests: tests/core/test_ledger.py, tests/daemon/test_reads.py.
- `fix(voice): the studio names Kokoro's size from the engine row the daemon reports, not a literal.` README 3.1, ADR 0028 (voice full r2, Kokoro size 360 vs 400): the engine heading and the install-step line read `about N MB` from kokoro.size_mb (rounded), and name no size when the engine or its size is null; the fixture is 360 as the daemon serves it. Tests: apps/desktop/src/modules/voice/model.test.ts, view.test.tsx.
- `test(voice): the gateway's 1009 close on an oversized frame is checked on the wire.` README 3.2 (voice full r2 robustness-9, test half): a masked text-frame header declaring MAX_FRAME_BYTES + 1 bytes, with no payload, makes the client see the socket close with code 1009; no source change. Test: tests/channels/test_voice_gateway.py.
- `fix(voice): spoken_line builds its cut footer with PromptBlock.footer(), not its own f-string.` README 2 invariant 4 (voice full r2 craft-9): the spoken text is byte-identical (the cap, a space, then `(showing N of M)`) and still truncated; the TTS: line and empty-turn paths are unchanged. Tests: the existing asserts in tests/channels/test_commands.py and tests/channels/test_voice_gateway.py.
- `fix(voice): the studio's comparison transcription writes its ledger row.` README 2 invariant 6, ADR 0083 amended (voice full r2 craft-3, ADR 0083 open item): POST /voice/transcribe?engine=openai writes one row (engine and model from the hearing meter, conversation empty, origin core, surface and trigger voice, no turn id; engine_error on a refusal, unknown on any other fault) through record_speech, the one helper the gateway's own speech rows now use too; a local engine, a refusal of the request and a transcriber that sent nothing write none.
- `fix(recall): the 'memory could not be read' note is swept of fence markers after it is made one line.` README 2 invariant 4, ADR 0076 amended (memory full r2 craft-9): unavailable_trace runs canonical_line then fence.neutralise, the order memory_line uses, so a marker in a SQLite message, whole or split by a zero-width character, is redacted; the note stays a plain line starting with UNAVAILABLE_PREFIX. Test: tests/core/test_recall.py (whole and split markers, failing at base).
- `fix(recall): around() finds a term's first match in the body itself, so a body whose lowercase is longer than it is still shows the match.` README 3.5, ADR 0042 (memory full r2 craft-10): the offsets came from body.lower() and sliced body, and U+0130 lowers to two code points; the first case-insensitive match is now searched in body, the rest of around() is unchanged. Test: tests/core/test_recall.py (300 U+0130 then a needle, failing at base).
- `fix(brain): a memory body's line endings are LF on disk, so a rebuild gives back the rows the writer indexed.` README 2 invariant 1 and 3.5, ADR 0003 amended (memory full r2 craft-6): append_episode, write_fact and write_procedural turn CRLF, then a lone CR, into LF before the strip and the empty check, so the file, content_hash, excerpt, FTS body and row agree with what the universal-newline readers return; a body with no CR is unchanged, the readers and the writer's no-translation write are unchanged, and the CRLF-on-disk test of 5e91fe4 is superseded. Tests: tests/core/test_brain_store.py, tests/core/test_brain_reconcile.py, tests/core/test_recall.py (the CR guard at the render seam).
- `fix(brain): a fact's expires_at, which no file carried and no read honoured, is removed from the writer and the rebuild.` README 3 and 8, ADR 0003 amended (memory full r2 craft-1): Brain.write_fact loses the expires_at parameter (a caller that passes it gets a TypeError before any file is written), the rebuild stops reading the key and its 'expires_at is not a single value' skip and _bindable are gone, so a fact file with an expires_at line of any shape is indexed and the key ignored; the companion_fact column stays for the Personas layout and SCHEMA_VERSION is unchanged. Tests: tests/core/test_brain_store.py, tests/core/test_brain_reconcile.py.
- `fix(voice): every request to a metered speech provider is a ledger row, failures included.` README 2 invariant 6 and 3.2, ADR 0083 (voice full r2 craft-3, economics-4): the gateway writes one row per transcription request and per spoken line a metered half was sent (engine `openai`, its stt or tts model, surface and trigger `voice`, the turn's conversation and origin, rounds 1, tokens 0, `cost_usd` None, a spoken line's row carrying the turn id), `engine_error` on a `VoiceBackendError`, not an error for a line cut off by barge-in; local engines, the scripted backend and an utterance too short to send write none; `backends.py` gains `Meter`, `hearing_meter`/`speaking_meter` (answered per half by `ComposedBackend`) and `BufferedTranscriber.requested`, and a write that raises is retried once, then logged in a fixed line; no schema, price or event change; tests in tests/channels/test_voice_gateway.py and tests/channels/test_voice_openai.py.
- `fix(voice): a line cut off by barge-in closes its synthesis at once, and a render cancelled before its process exists is killed when it starts.` README 3.2, ADR 0019 amended (voice full r2 craft-6): _speak_loop iterates the backend's synthesize and closes it in a finally, so its own finally runs before voice.stopped is sent, and KokoroTTS._render kills the process as soon as it is assigned when the job was cancelled in between; tests in tests/channels/test_voice_gateway.py and tests/channels/test_voice_engines.py.
- `fix(voice): what a voice session keeps for the life of its socket is bounded.` README 3.2, ADR 0019 amended (voice full r2 economics-6, craft-7): KokoroTTS.started keeps the newest STARTED_KEPT = 32 sentences, VoiceSession.cards at most MAX_SESSION_CARDS = 32 newest first (a trimmed pending card falls under ADR 0073's inbox rule, and a bare yes still answers the newest), and the panel's Player forgets a dropped generation once its voice.stopped is handled; tests in tests/channels/test_voice_engines.py, tests/channels/test_voice_gateway.py and apps/desktop/src/stores/voice.test.ts.
- `fix(catalog): core.write_fact refuses before its card what the writer would refuse after it, and any param it would ignore.` README 3.3 and 2 invariant 2, ADR 0082 (provenance-backed-memory full r2 craft-7): core.write_fact now refuses, before its card, a key with a line break, a value that is only whitespace, and any param it would ignore, with `validator_failed` naming the param and never echoing the value; `WRITE_FACT_SCHEMA` gets `additionalProperties: False`, `validators.fact_text()` runs before the sources are checked, and core.recall and core.checkpoint stay open; tests in tests/core/test_catalog.py, tests/core/test_validators.py and tests/test_wiring.py.
- `fix(voice): a provider reply is read up to a ceiling, so a transcript or a spoken line cannot grow without bound.` README 3.2, ADR 0019 amended (voice full r2 economics-7): transcribe reads at most MAX_TRANSCRIPTION_REPLY_BYTES (1 MiB) plus one and refuses a larger reply before parsing, synthesize stops at MAX_SPEECH_REPLY_SECONDS (180) of PCM without yielding the chunk that crosses it; ADR 0019 also corrects the craft-2 memory bound to 6 x 4 MiB per socket; tests in tests/channels/test_voice_openai.py.
- `fix(voice): every download the installer makes stops at its pinned size and within one install's time limit.` README 3.1, ADR 0078 amended (voice full r2 economics-5): Sources.size and pinned_size beside the digests (nine sizes, the six model sizes from the Hugging Face tree API's lfs.size), a differing Content-Length refused before a byte is written, a body past its pin stopped within one chunk, DOWNLOAD_DEADLINE_S = 45 min per run from the lock; tests in tests/channels/test_voice_install.py.
- `fix(brain): a damaged index stops the brain with a typed error naming the repair, and athena brain reconcile sets it aside and rebuilds.` README 2 invariant 1 and 3.5, ADR 0081 (provenance-backed-memory full r2 robustness-1): IndexDamagedError names the repair, `athena brain reconcile` sets the damaged index aside as index.sqlite.damaged-<UTC> and rebuilds, and a locked index is not called damaged; tests in tests/core/test_brain_store.py and tests/test_cli.py.
- `fix(brain): a memory file is written whole or not at all, and a string that is not valid Unicode is refused before a card is filed.` README 2 invariant 1 and 3.5, ADR 0080 (provenance-backed-memory full r2 robustness-2, craft-8): the file is written to a temp, fsynced and replaced, and the validator refuses a string that is not valid Unicode; tests in tests/core/test_brain_store.py, tests/core/test_validators.py and tests/test_wiring.py.
- `fix(voice): transcription runs on the worker, so the reader keeps reading while an utterance is transcribed.` README 3.2, ADR 0019 amended (voice full r2 craft-2): `_stop` keeps its order (none or refused, then the ADR 0074 cap check) and enqueues a job carrying the transcriber; the worker finishes it, sends the same engine_error on a VoiceBackendError, and starts no turn for a closed socket; a waiting job now holds its PCM, at most 5 x 4 MiB per socket; tests in tests/channels/test_voice_gateway.py (an unknown frame answered while finish() is blocked, a refusal then a good utterance, a stop waiting behind a turn never transcribed after close).
- `fix(voice): the OpenAI backend maps a provider reply cut short to VoiceBackendError.` README 3.2, ADR 0019 amended (voice full r2 robustness-3, the IncompleteRead case, and the voice gateway fix's question on backends.py): transcribe and synthesize catch http.client.HTTPException beside the other transport errors, raising 'transcription failed: <TypeName>' or 'synthesis failed: <TypeName>' from None; tests in tests/channels/test_voice_openai.py (a synthesis cut after one chunk, a transcription cut), both failing at base.
- `docs(catalog): core.write_fact tells the model a citable id is in a recalled episode's label, with an end-to-end test and ADR 0079.` README §2 invariant 2, ADR 0079 (memory full r2 value-4): only the core.write_fact description changed, no schema, validator, class or cap; test in tests/test_wiring.py (a fact written with the id taken from the user's line of core.recall).
- `feat(recall): a recalled episode line starts with its id and role, so the model can cite the user's own words.` README §2 invariant 2, ADR 0079, memory full r2 value-4; tests: tests/core/test_recall.py (label, roles, machine, unreadable role, distilled line), tests/test_wiring.py (core.recall labels, cap).
- `fix(voice): the worker logs the traceback of a turn that raises under the fixed message 'voice: a turn failed', with no user or page text, before it sends the same turn.error unknown with the exception's type name.` README 3.2, ADR 0019 amended (voice full r2 robustness-7 and craft-10); test in tests/channels/test_voice_gateway.py (a turn raising KeyError: turn.error detail KeyError, one ERROR record with exc_info, the next utterance still runs).
- `fix(voice): the speaker thread outlives a synthesis failure: any Exception raised while a line is spoken ends that line with voice.stopped reason error, logs its traceback once naming the generation and never the text, and the next line is spoken.` README 3.2, ADR 0019 amended (voice full r2 robustness-3): a VoiceBackendError keeps its reason without a traceback; tests in tests/channels/test_voice_gateway.py (a raising synthesize, a generator that raises after a chunk, an engine refusal logs nothing).
- `fix(desktop): openMicrophone stops every track of its stream, closes a context it created, and rethrows the original error when audioContext, createMediaStreamSource or createScriptProcessor throws after getUserMedia, so a failed open no longer leaves the microphone live.` README 3.1, ADR 0020 amended (voice full r2 robustness-5): the store's catch still shows `microphone: ...`; tests in apps/desktop/src/lib/voice.test.ts (three failure points, and the happy-path stop)
- `fix(desktop): a window blur or a hidden document ends a voice press that is still waiting as well as one that is listening, so the microphone is never opened after the key is up.` README 3.1, ADR 0020 amended (voice full r2 robustness-4): the press goes stale like a keyup, sending no start and opening no microphone; a blur while listening still sends stop; tests in apps/desktop/src/stores/voice.test.ts (a blur and a hidden document during captureOf, a blur when idle)
- `fix(desktop): a typed line stops when its voice socket closed while it waited for the tab capture, so the refusal's reason stays shown and nothing is sent to a closed socket.` README 3.1, ADR 0074 (the 2026-10-11 amendment's panel rough edge; council full r2 economics-1 follow-up): after the publish, the connect and the capture `say` continues only while its socket is still the store's open one, and sets no phase, keeps the reason and sends nothing otherwise; tests in apps/desktop/src/stores/voice.test.ts cover a 1013 refusal and a plain close during the capture.
- `fix(voice): every archive and whisper model the installer fetches must match a SHA-256 pinned beside its URL, hashed as it arrives, before anything is extracted or moved into the engine home; a mismatch deletes the download and fails the run naming the file, a URL with no pin is never fetched, and the whisper models are pinned to a Hugging Face commit instead of main.` README 3.1, ADR 0078 extending ADR 0028 (council full r2 craft-1, robustness-6): nine digests, the three archives from the GitHub release API's asset digests and the six models from the Hugging Face tree API's LFS sha256 at commit 5359861c, the sherpa engine and whisper-bin-x64.zip cross-checked by download, read 2026-10-11; the Content-Length check stays; tests in tests/channels/test_voice_install.py.
- `fix(voice): one daemon admits at most MAX_SESSIONS = 2 voice sockets at once, each holding its admission until its reader has returned and its worker has finished the turn it was running, and a socket past that gets one turn.error validator_failed naming the limit and is closed 1013 with no thread started.` README 3.1, ADR 0074 amended (council full r2 economics-1, the width across sockets): the bound is 2 sockets × 5 turns × 9 lane turns = 90 metered lane turns and 2 transcriptions at once; the admission is given back exactly once on every close, 1011 and a reader that dies by exception included; server.py and the panel are unchanged, and the panel does not reconnect on a 1013; tests in tests/channels/test_voice_gateway.py.
- `fix(voice): a sentence reaches Kokoro's narrow command line with every character the child's ANSI code page cannot carry exactly folded to its NFKC form, else its NFKD form without combining marks, else one space, so a fullwidth quote or backslash can no longer split the quoted positional.` README 3.1, ADR 0069 amended a second time (council full r2 robustness-2): a character is carried when encoding it to the code page without best fit and decoding it back gives it again, the code page is GetACP() on Windows and the rule is the identity elsewhere, a carried word never changes and the one leading space stays; tests in tests/channels/test_voice_engines.py run the council's two sentences and every BMP code point through list2cmdline, best fit and CommandLineToArgvW under 1250, 1252 and the running code page.
- `fix(voice): a tool_result is kept only for a call this socket sent and still waits on; an answer to any other call id, or one that arrives after the wait gave up, is dropped with a debug line that never carries its output.` README 3.1, ADR 0010, ADR 0074 (council full r2 economics-2): `_results` no longer grows with answers nobody waits for; an answer that lands before the wait begins is still collected; tests in tests/channels/test_voice_gateway.py cover 2,000 unrequested results held nowhere, an early answer collected and a late one dropped.
- `fix(voice): a turn that has not started when the socket closes never starts: the turns still waiting are dropped, the worker skips one it had already taken, and no continuation step begins after the close, while a turn already inside turn_events runs to its end.` README 3.1, ADR 0012, ADR 0074 (council full r2 economics-1, the closed-socket half; the width across sockets is a later line): tests in tests/channels/test_voice_gateway.py cover four turns queued behind a held one reaching no lane after close, and an answer after close starting no continuation step.
- `fix(voice): a control frame json cannot decode, nested too deep included, is refused with parse_error and the socket stays open, and the 1011 door sets its close code before it builds its log line, whose frame label never raises.` README 3.1, ADR 0019 (council full r2 robustness-1): 100,000 `[` no longer closes 1000 with nothing logged; tests in tests/channels/test_voice_gateway.py cover two undecodable depths with the socket open and a raising handler closing 1011 with one log record.
- `fix(memory): a number that is not finite, NaN, inf or -inf, is refused by the schema validator with validator_failed, and Brain.write_fact and write_procedural raise ValueError for a non-finite confidence before the file is written, so a NaN confidence no longer leaves a file with no row.` README §2 invariant 1, ADR 0003 (council full r1 robustness-3): the message is `<path>: <value> is not a finite number`; the executor's existing ValueError branch makes it validator_failed; tests in tests/core/test_validators.py, tests/core/test_brain_store.py and tests/test_wiring.py.
- `fix(brain): a rebuild names a fact or procedural whose confidence is an integer too large for a float, such as 1 followed by 400 zeros, as not a number and indexes the rest, instead of aborting on OverflowError.` README §2 invariant 1, ADR 0003 (council full r1 robustness-2): the message is the existing `<rel>: confidence <value> is not a number` and the file leaves no row; tests in tests/core/test_brain_reconcile.py.
- `fix(catalog): neutralise_text also drops the variation selectors U+FE00-FE0F and U+E0100-E01EF, wherever it already dropped control and format characters: the capabilities block and both memory lanes.` README §2 invariant 6, ADR 0068 amended (injection-resistance full r2 robustness-3, craft-1): every other Mn character, a combining accent among them, is kept, and fence bodies are untouched; tests in tests/core/test_catalog.py.
- `perf(brain): each companion_fts row has its companion_node row's rowid and a write deletes and inserts by it, instead of deleting by the UNINDEXED node_id, which scanned the whole index; companion_node is written by an upsert that keeps its rowid, and an index at schema version 1 or unstamped is re-keyed to version 2 when a Brain opens it.` README §2 invariant 1, ADR 0077 (council full r1 economics-2): one write is 242 SQLite VM steps at 100 nodes and at 2,000, where it was 1,446 and 22,346; FTS rows with no node are dropped, nothing on disk changes and index_fingerprint is unchanged; tests in tests/core/test_brain_store.py.
- `perf(memory): core.recall reads one page of matched episodes, at most limit (EPISODE_WINDOW = 20 when none is given) written by a person or a page and at most MACHINE_EPISODE_SLOTS = 2 machine ones, in two LIMITed selects merged in the old order, instead of every matching body.` README §2 invariant 4, ADR 0058 (council full r1 economics-1): the packed answer is the same as before for every limit, and M still counts every match; with no limit, more than 20 short matches under the cap now give 20; tests in tests/core/test_recall.py.
- `fix(memory): a memory store that cannot be read is reported, never read as nothing matched: the first sqlite3 error ends the recall, every block says memory could not be read with SQLite's message, the turn still runs on it, and core.recall fails with engine_error; a query's control characters, NUL included, are dropped before quoting.` README §2 invariant 4, ADR 0076 (council full r1 robustness-1, robustness-9's NUL query): `RecallTrace.unavailable` tells it apart from an honest empty result, one warning is logged per recall, a lock costs one busy timeout instead of one per statement; tests in tests/core/test_recall.py and tests/test_wiring.py.
- `fix(memory): a fact or procedural enters the keyword lane as one quoted, neutralised line, as in the always tier, and both lanes now drop control and format characters before redacting fence markers, so a marker split by U+200B no longer reassembles.` README §2 invariant 6, ADR 0075, ADR 0071 amended (council full r1 robustness-4, robustness-5; injection-resistance full r2 robustness-1, craft-8): `recall.memory_line` canonicalises, sweeps, then caps (`KEYWORD_LINE_CAP` = 500) and quotes; an episode renders unchanged in every block; tests in tests/core/test_recall.py and tests/core/test_prompt.py.
- `fix(voice): a voice socket has at most MAX_PENDING_TURNS = 4 turns waiting behind the running one; a text frame or a stop past that gets one turn.error validator_failed saying so, a text frame interrupts and queues nothing, a stop is never transcribed or billed, and the socket stays open.` README 3.1, ADR 0074, ADR 0019 (council full r1 economics-2): the queue itself stays unbounded so the closing sentinel fits; tests in tests/channels/test_voice_gateway.py.
- `fix(voice): the panel's VoiceSocket catches a text frame it cannot decode, an unknown event kind included, and reports it as a close reason naming the kind, then closes the socket; onEvent is not called and onmessage no longer throws.` README 3.1, ADR 0019 (council full r1 robustness-10): the store is told and leaves thinking instead of waiting on a throw nothing caught; the existing test keeps its title and asserts the new behaviour; tests in apps/desktop/src/lib/voice.test.ts.
- `fix(voice): an archive member with a colon in any part of its name is refused, not only in the first, and the sherpa, Kokoro and whisper writers each check that the resolved destination is inside their engine home, raising InstallError naming the member and writing nothing.` README 3.1, ADR 0028 (council full r1 robustness-9): a name such as espeak-ng-data/D:\evil.txt no longer lands at D:evil.txt on Windows; tests in tests/channels/test_voice_install.py.
- `fix(voice): a WebSocket message is at most MAX_FRAGMENTS = 1,024 data frames and recv keeps a running byte total, so a flood of empty continuation frames costs linear time and raises FrameTooLarge, which closes 1009.` README 3.1, ADR 0019 (council full r1 robustness-7): the byte ceiling is unchanged and control frames do not count; tests in tests/channels/test_ws.py.
- `fix(voice): each push-to-talk press takes a token that a release, a socket close or a turn.error while listening invalidates, so a press whose key is already up sends no start and a microphone that resolves late is stopped and never kept; a window blur or a hidden page while listening acts as the key coming up.` README 3.1, ADR 0020 (council full r1 robustness-4): the microphone no longer stays open under a phase of thinking or idle; tests in apps/desktop/src/stores/voice.test.ts.
