- `fix(demo-kit): a wheel or pinch past a zoom bound no longer slides the scene, because the anchored zoom clamps before it solves the pan.` docs/kit-camera-contract.md §1-2 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): ten wheel ticks at the minimum zoom moved the pan 600 scene units with the zoom unchanged; `zoomAt` takes the bounds' zoom range and the rig passes it. Test: camera.test.ts, two bound cases, seeded red.
- `fix(atlas): the Home key on the lane canvas flies home, not to the sheet's top-left corner.` docs/kit-camera-contract.md (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): the rig's own Home flies to the kit's rest pose (pan 0,0), the world's corner; one `flyHome` (poseFor level 0) now serves the toolbar and the canvas key. Test: new `test/lanes.drawing.test.ts`.
- `fix(atlas): the lane canvas's label says its arrow keys pan and names the zoom and Home keys, instead of promising node-to-node arrows.` kit camera key map (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): on the focused canvas the arrows pan; they rove only from a node. Test: lanes.drawing canvas-label case.
- `fix(motion): a frame subscriber that throws is dropped and logged, and the window's other motion keeps running.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): one throw used to escape the frame callback before it rescheduled, freezing every animation in the window; each subscriber call is now guarded, a thrower is dropped like a settled one and named once. Test: motion.test.ts.
- `fix(demo-kit): the kit's default curve is typed once, parsed by the one bezier parser, and pinned to the template's --dk-ease.` formula §1 rule 4 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): camera.ts typed the curve twice beside a second bezier parser and a seeded retune of either copy failed no test; the easing now lives in tokens.ts on parseBezier and a test reads the template's stylesheet. Test: tokens.test.ts, three new cases, seeded red.
- `fix(desktop): a daemon stream the panel stops reading early is cancelled, not left open and unread.` README 3.2 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): `sseFrames` cancels a body that had not ended before releasing its lock, so a `break` on the terminal event or a throw mid-turn closes the connection; a body that ended on its own is untouched. Test: api.test.ts.
- `fix(demo-kit): echo-rule's private same-place test is pinned to sameFocus over every pair of foci, so the deliberate copy cannot drift.` docs/kit-camera-contract.md §4 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist): the copy is deliberate (node's ESM resolver refuses an extensionless value import, probed), but a seeded drift of sameFocus failed no test; 729 pairs now hold the two together. Test: echo.test.ts, seeded red.
- `fix(desktop): reading an app id back to its host returns null for an escape that is not a code point, instead of throwing.` ADR 0065, ADR 0099 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): `hostOfDerivedId` threw `RangeError` past U+10FFFF and read `_x1g_` as U+0001; an escape is now 1-6 hex digits naming a real code point, else null. Test: manifest.test.ts.
- `fix(desktop): the store tables, the daemon status and the tab the panel restates are held to the Rust that defines them.` README 3.5 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist): `lib/rust-mirrors.test.ts` reads `store.rs` TABLES, `daemon.rs` DaemonStatus and Health, and `tabs.rs` Tab, and pins `STORE_TABLES`, `DaemonStatus`, `DaemonHealth` and `Tab` to them; seeded red by dropping one of each. Test: rust-mirrors.test.ts.
- `fix(desktop): a hands catalogue the shell could not give is logged, not swallowed.` README 3.4, ADR 0065 (scan-sweep stabilize, observability-auditor): `startHands` logs a failed `hands_list` once instead of a bare catch, since an empty catalogue silently leaves every page without hands; the next start still retries. Test: hands.test.ts.
- `fix(demo-kit): the rig exposes its frame, and semantic zoom hands it to poseFor and both resolvers.` docs/kit-camera-contract.md §3 (scan-sweep stabilize, ux-reviewer): atlas KIT-GAPS R4-5; `rig.frame()` is the box the anchored zoom already measures, and the camera-drives-nav decision is now the pure, tested `cameraMove`. Test: semantic.test.ts, two new cases; probe 0 of 4 calls carried a frame before, 4 of 4 after.
- `fix(demo-kit): a + or - key press during a fly stops the fly, as an arrow, a drag and the wheel already do.` docs/kit-camera-contract.md §2 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): the fly's next frame overwrote the key's zoom (probe: "+" at 1.65 still landed at 3.89); the mapping is now the pure `keyAction` and every key move halts the fly first. Test: gesture.test.ts key mapping; probe before/after.
- `fix(demo-kit): the camera leaves a key alone when a field, a child or the browser owns it.` WCAG 1.4.4, zoom/escape.ts ownership rule (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): keys typed into a field inside the scene zoomed the camera and never reached the field, a roving list's arrow also orbited, and Ctrl+= was swallowed; `keyAction` now declines all three. Test: gesture.test.ts ownership case, seeded red; probe before/after.
- `fix(demo-kit): an overlay chooses where focus returns on the frame it restores it, so an origin card put back by the same commit is found.` formula §1 rule 5, WCAG 2.4.3 (scan-sweep stabilize, state-coverage): `returnFocusTo()` ran in the cleanup, before the level underneath had put its card back, and focus dropped to the body (probe); the target is now resolved inside the frame. Probe before/after.
- `fix(demo-kit): the first echo of a session gets the token's safety net, not the 400 ms default.` formula §1 rule 4 (scan-sweep stabilize, state-coverage): useEcho read the budget in a layout effect before useLevelFlight's passive effect had resolved it, so the first echo's net was 400 ms whatever the token said (probe: 400 vs 1200); the budget is now read when asked for. Probe before/after.
- `fix(demo-kit): the zoom barrel no longer sends a reader to a ../three/nav module and a /three barrel that do not exist.` examples/demo-kit README export table (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): two comments named paths with zero files and no export behind them; they now say the WebGL barrel is gone and why the model lives here.
- `fix(atlas): set_lens accepts the "none" its description tells an agent to pass to clear the lens.` README §3.4 tier 1 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): the enum held only concept ids, so the kit's door refused "none" before the handler's clear branch could run and no agent could clear the lens; the parameter moved to tools/read.ts and carries the clear word. Test: test/shell.test.ts.
- `fix(cli): athena doctor and reconcile print their JSON whatever code page the pipe has, so a brain under a non-ASCII home no longer ends in a traceback.` README §7 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): `_emit` dumped with `ensure_ascii=False`, and a Windows pipe is cp1252, so a brain path holding a letter cp1252 cannot spell raised half-way through the object (exit 1, half an object, a traceback); the object is now ASCII-escaped. Test: tests/test_cli.py.
- `fix(atlas): the variant's loading fallback says it is loading, not that the drawing is "not built yet".` async-ui-states/placeholder-design (scan-sweep stabilize, state-coverage): one card served the Suspense fallback and the error boundary, so every cold load announced that Lanes did not exist; the card now takes a loading or failed state, and only failed cites the file and offers Try again. Test: test/shell.test.ts.
- `fix(atlas): the L2 pane's narrow layout applies on a narrow screen; its query named a container nothing declares.` DESIGN.md breakpoints (scan-sweep stabilize, ui-perfectionist): `@container at-plate` and `@container at-app` named containers no stylesheet declares, so the pane kept its desktop insets on a phone; the pane's rule is a @media query with its grow origin adjusted, and the mast's dead duplicate is gone. Test: test/shell.test.ts.
- `fix(contracts): the desktop's adopt wire types are held to contracts/adopt.py field for field and word for word, not only kind for kind.` ADR 0097 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): the parity test compared only event and leg kinds, so a field or a vocabulary member on one side only stayed green; it now reads each event's fields, the eight nested shapes and the seven vocabularies off adopt.ts. Seeded drifts caught: 0 of 4 before, 4 of 4 after. Test: tests/contracts/test_adopt_parity.py.
- `fix(catalog): the capabilities footer counts the names this render could address, not a switched-off app's or another lane's.` prompt-assembly/capability-documentation (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `render_capabilities` filtered by lane and disabled origin but took `M` from the whole catalog, so switching an app off announced "(showing 3 of 5)" with nothing cut, and a real cut beside it did not add up; `M` is now the entries this render could list. Test: tests/core/test_catalog.py.
- `fix(cli): a missing law's doctor line names every place it looked and the variable that fixes it, even under a home with ". " in its name.` ADR 0006 (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): the constitution stage kept only the text before the first ". ", which dropped the `$ATHENA_CONSTITUTION` remedy and cut a path like `J. Smith` in half; the detail is now the loader's whole message. Test: tests/test_cli.py.
- `fix(cli): athena brain reconcile exits non-zero when its report says ok is false, so a skipped memory is not a clean rebuild to a script.` ADR 0003 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): the report said `ok: false` for a skipped file and the command still exited 0, unlike its own damaged-index refusal and the doctor; the exit code now follows `ok`. Test: tests/test_cli.py.
- `fix(recall): a query matches what a memory says, not the role or kind tags the index files it under.` ADR 0042 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): every MATCH searched the FTS `tags` column too, so "user" in a question matched every turn the user typed and "fact" matched every fact; a whole recall for one Acme turn showed "(showing 20 of 41)". Each MATCH is now held to the `body` column; after, "(showing 1 of 1)". Test: tests/core/test_recall.py.
- `fix(atlas): the shell's stylesheets drop 31 classes no component renders, rails.css whole.` dead-code/deletion-protocols (scan-sweep stabilize, code-optimizer): the round-4 legend and turn bar, the round-5 render switch, the reading line and the foot had no renderer (whole-token search of app/, components/, lib/); 512 lines go. Test: test/shell.test.ts fails on a styled class nothing renders.
- `fix(atlas): the token file declares only roles something reads: twelve orphaned tokens go.` design-tokens/token-taxonomy (scan-sweep stabilize, visual-craft): `--at-cyan`, the four `--at-grid*`, `--at-turnbar-h`, `--at-tick`, `--at-present`, `--at-port`, `--at-hair`, `--at-cs` and `--at-dur-beat` had no `var()` and no script reader; 72 tokens declared become 60, the raw ramps untouched. Test: test/shell.test.ts.
- `fix(validators): the gate checks a list of types and refuses a type it cannot check, where it used to skip both and admit any value.` mcp-tools/tool-schema-design, gate-sees-target (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): `params_schema_problems` lets a property declare `type: ["string", "null"]`, but `_check_value` checked a type only when it was one known string, so a list, a typo such as `"strng"` or an `items` type of `5` let an object, a number or a list through to a card or an AUTO call; each name is now checked (with `null`), and any other `type` refuses every call as a malformed schema. Test: tests/core/test_validators.py.
- `fix(wiring): a composition that fails after opening the brain closes it, and closing an Athena closes the brain whatever the adopt studio or the vault raised.` README §3.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): `build_local` left the brain it opened open when a later step raised (the proving world builds an Athena in a process that lives on, and on Windows the open index cannot be moved), and `AthenaLocal.close` skipped the brain when the adopt studio raised; both now close it. Test: tests/test_wiring.py.
- `fix(atlas): the L2 pane's claim chips say whether they are the lens, as the claims rail's rows already do.` WCAG 4.1.2 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): both call the same toggling setLens, but the chip showed its state by colour only; it now carries aria-pressed. Test: test/shell.test.ts.
- `fix(atlas): set_view publishes no enum while there are no views, so its own "no views" answer is reachable.` variants/viewBus.ts contract (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): an empty enum made the kit's door refuse every value ("must be one of , got") before the variant's module answered; setViewParameters drops it, and the handler says what it knows. Test: test/shell.test.ts.
- `fix(contracts): the id and channel contracts stop citing a TypeScript id table, a parity test and an MCP renderer that do not exist.` AGENTS.md ids rule (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): ids.py named `lib/ids.ts` and `tests/test_ids_parity.py` (neither exists), channel.py an MCP renderer that is not built, and docs/features/core.md the same parity test; each now says what is there, including that the shell's capture id is held to the table by no test.
- `fix(brain): a question's punctuation is not part of its words, so "deadline?" finds the window around "deadline" and "it?" no longer matches every turn.` ADR 0042 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `fts_terms` kept a word's edge punctuation, which FTS5 drops, so the excerpt window searched for `deadline?` and showed a long episode's head (3 of 4 phrasings), and `it?` passed the short-word filter and pushed 5 of 5 recent turns out of the window. Words lose their edge punctuation first; after, 4 of 4 and 5 of 5. Test: tests/core/test_recall.py.
- `fix(contracts): the hand marker and the side-effects vocabulary are held to the TypeScript and the bridge that spell them by hand.` README §3.3, ADR 0066 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `HAND_RUNNER` (copied in lib/hands.ts) and `SIDE_EFFECTS` (copied in gate.js) had no cross-language test, so a drift left both sides green; tests/contracts/test_manifest.py now reads both declarations. Seeded drifts caught: 0 of 3 before, 3 of 3 after.
- `fix(atlas): the pane's chips, links and Close and the placeholder's Try again ease into hover on the hair clock, as the mast and rail do.` DESIGN.md §7 (scan-sweep stabilize, ui-perfectionist): four of seven hovered shell controls changed colour in one frame; each now transitions what its :hover changes on `--at-dur-hair`. Test: test/shell.test.ts.
- `fix(contracts): a decision card's option is read by the event's own rule - a later field dropped, a malformed option a ValueError - not by whatever DecisionOption(**o) raises.` README §3.2 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): `event_from_dict` dropped unknown keys from events but built options with `DecisionOption(**o)`, so a later option field raised TypeError and `{"id": 7}` passed as an int id; options now drop unknown keys and refuse a malformed one with ValueError. Test: tests/contracts/test_channel.py.
- `fix(atlas): the variant placeholder centres itself in the stage; its grid placement was inert in a block parent.` sheet.css (scan-sweep stabilize, visual-craft): `place-self`/`grid-row` were written for the deleted `.at-stage-in` grid, so the loading and failure card sat top-left under the vignette's dark edge; it now centres by its own box. Test: test/shell.test.ts.
- `fix(cli): the version athena --version prints is held to the one pyproject.toml ships the package as.` README §7 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist): `__version__` and pyproject's `version` are two hand-kept copies and the smoke test only checked the first had two dots; it now reads pyproject.toml and compares. Test: tests/test_smoke.py.
- `fix(atlas): the shell's header comments name the files, counts and mechanism that exist.` AGENTS.md "every module cites its section" (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): ten stale claims (canvas/canvas.css, Atlas.tsx as the pane's writer, a shared id and a matrix3d face the pane never had, six sections of five, one variant of three, eight modules of nineteen) now say what the tree holds. Comments only.
- `fix(recall): fleet chatter that outranks a relevant turn no longer pushes it out of the frame window.` ref §8 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): a whole recall reads person-written and machine matches as two pages, but the frame window read one page of `budget * 2` by rank and capped machine rows only afterwards, so 45 outranking fleet-event rows hid the one relevant turn. The window now reads the same two pages; after, the turn is shown beside exactly two machine rows. Test: tests/core/test_recall.py.
- `fix(atlas): the variant boundary drops a reset branch its key makes unreachable, and the test pins the key instead.` dead-code/deletion-protocols (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): the boundary is keyed by slug and attempt at its one render site, so its componentDidUpdate slug check could never fire; the key is now what the test guards. Test: test/shell.test.ts.
- `fix(approvals): the loser of two answers racing on one card is refused like a second click, not handed the winner's row as its own.` hitl-approval/resume-after-decision (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): `resolve` ignored its conditional UPDATE's rowcount, so a decline that lost the race to an approve returned the approved row without error and the lane went on to replay the gate for the surface that said decline; the loser now gets the same "is approved, not pending" refusal a sequential second answer gets. Test: tests/core/test_approvals.py.
- `fix(atlas): the mast's model counts reach a screen reader; two of the three are said nowhere else.` WCAG 1.3.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): the systems, components and edges figures were drawn and aria-hidden; systems and edges appear nowhere else on the surface. Test: test/shell.test.ts.
- `fix(recall): a whole recall that found no distilled memory reserves its note, so the answer fits its cap and the gate never cuts the footer.` ADR 0058 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): packing reserved the header, footer and keyword block but not the "(no distilled memory matched the query)" line `core.recall` renders after them, so 3 of 86 episode sizes went 10 to 28 chars past RECALL_CAP and the gate cut the note and the footer. The note's cost is now spent before packing; after, 0 of 86. Test: tests/core/test_recall.py.
- `fix(validators): an enum compares values as JSON does, so true no longer passes where the schema lists 1.` mcp-tools/tool-schema-design (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): the enum check was Python's `in`, where `True == 1` and `False == 0`, so an untyped enum of numbers, or a list or object holding one, admitted a boolean the page never offered; a boolean now equals only a boolean, numbers still compare by value (1.0 matches 1), lists and objects member by member. Test: tests/core/test_validators.py.
- `fix(brain): a memory file saved with a byte order mark is rebuilt, not skipped as having "no id in frontmatter".` ADR 0003 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): an editor that saves UTF-8 with a BOM put U+FEFF before the `---`, so the rebuild could not see the header and dropped the memory from the index under a reason that named the wrong cause. `frontmatter.parse` drops one leading BOM; no file is rewritten. After, the probe brain rebuilds with 0 skipped. Tests: tests/core/test_brain_frontmatter.py, tests/core/test_brain_reconcile.py.
- `fix(constitution): a law.md holding nothing but whitespace is no law, so the loader looks on and the doctor fails instead of reporting a law found.` ADR 0006 (scan-sweep stabilize, risk-assessor): `Source.complete` asked only whether `law.md` existed, so a zero-byte copy loaded, the doctor said ok, and every prompt opened with "(no law on file)"; an empty law now falls through to the next candidate like a missing one, and with none left `load` raises `ConstitutionMissing`. Test: tests/core/test_constitution.py.
- `fix(brain): the tree and the rebuild's walk are built from the scope lists the writers accept, so a new scope cannot be written and then vanish on the next rebuild.` ADR 0003 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist): the scopes were three hand-kept lists (the writers' guard, `paths.TREE`, `reconcile.WALK`), and a scope seeded into the first alone was written, never walked, and lost on rebuild with every test green. The lists live in `paths` and the tree and walk derive from them; seeded drifts caught 0 of 1 before, impossible after. Test: tests/core/test_brain_reconcile.py.
- `fix(ledger): the documented triggers are the ones the writers use, and the reason rule says it drops rather than refuses.` cost-metering/spend-attribution (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): `TRIGGERS` listed `chat`, `mcp`, `schedule` and `resume`, none of which any writer stores, while every typed turn is `cli` and cards and adopt runs write `decision` and `adopt.*`; and the module said `record` refuses a success with a reason, which it drops. The list is now held to `TurnContext.trigger` and `BrowserLane.decision_trigger` by a test. Test: tests/core/test_ledger.py.
- `fix(recall): a long turn the frame window shows by recency, or by a match the text spells differently, is marked where it was cut.` ADR 0042 (scan-sweep stabilize, observability-auditor): the recency tail rendered the stored 500-byte excerpt and `around()` fell back to it when no word matched literally, so a long turn reached the model cut with no mark and read as whole. Both now window the body and end a cut with " …"; an uncut turn is unchanged. Test: tests/core/test_recall.py.
- `fix(brain): a COMMIT that fails is rolled back, so the writer does not hold every later write in a transaction no reader ever sees.` ADR 0003 (scan-sweep stabilize, risk-assessor): `write_txn` rolled back a failing body but not a failing COMMIT, which can leave the transaction open; every later write then joined it as an "outer frame", was never committed, and was invisible to readers until close dropped it. A failed COMMIT is now rolled back before it is raised. Test: tests/core/test_brain_store.py.
- `fix(recall): a fact or rule longer than its stored excerpt is marked as cut in the always and keyword lines.` ADR 0075 (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): both distilled lanes rendered the unmarked 500-byte stored excerpt, and the line cap's ellipsis never fired under it, so a long fact (or any fact in a three-byte script) read as whole. They now window the body and mark the cut; unmarked long lines 3 of 4 before, 0 of 4 after. Test: tests/core/test_recall.py.
- `fix(brain): damage the session upsert meets first is named IndexDamagedError and the handle is closed, so reconcile can set the index aside.` ADR 0081 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): the constructor's damage guard covered the schema but not the session upsert after it, which is the first statement to read a data page, so a damaged session table escaped as a bare DatabaseError with the writer still open (Windows then refuses the set-aside rename). The upsert is inside the guard. Test: tests/core/test_brain_store.py.
- `test(brain): the two reconcile tests that pinned exit 0 on a skipped file now pin exit 1, as cmd_reconcile does since 30435c7.` ADR 0003 (scan-sweep wave 3 integration): the CLI round made the exit code say what the report's `ok` says, and its scoped gate did not run `tests/core`, so the combined-tree gate went red on these two; their skipped-file and `ok: false` assertions are unchanged.
- `fix(store): the shell mints capture ids at the contract's twelve hex digits, so the daemon keeps them and a card can show its screenshot.` README §3.5 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `store.rs` minted `cap_` + 16 hex while `contracts/ids.py` accepts 12, so `capture_id_from` dropped every shell-minted capture and no card carried its shot. The shell moved, not the contract (every kind is 12, every fixture is 12, and no card ever held a 16-hex id, so nothing is orphaned). `tests/contracts/test_ids.py` now reads `store.rs` with comments stripped and holds its prefix and width to the table.
- `fix(wiring): the daemon is not built when no law is on file, so athena serve refuses on its failure line instead of composing every turn under "(no law on file)".` README §3.1, ADR 0006 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, assigned from core-gate-and-ledger): `build_local` loaded the constitution with `load_or_empty`, the doctor's and fixtures' non-raising view, while `core/constitution.py` says a turn with no law should not have started and the doctor fails the same state; it now calls `load`, so `ConstitutionMissing` (where the loader looked, and `$ATHENA_CONSTITUTION`) reaches the failure line the shell shows as "the daemon refused to start", with the brain closed again. Tests: `tests/test_wiring.py` (the build raises and closes its brain; a built Athena carries a law) and `tests/daemon/test_ready.py` (a real `serve` with an empty `$ATHENA_CONSTITUTION` prints the failure line), both red before.
- `fix(demo-kit): the WebMCP door refuses an array longer than the maxItems its schema publishes, instead of handing it to a handler that slices it.` README §3.4, design 5.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor; mcp-tools/tool-schema-design): `parametersToJsonSchema` publishes `maxItems` on every capped array and the bridge's `inject.js` enforces it, but `door.ts` never read it, so a native browser agent reaching the page without the bridge got an over-cap id list silently cut while the tool reported success. `examples/demo-kit/test/ui-webmcp.test.ts` pins the top-level and nested cases.
- `fix(daemon): a daemon that refuses to start keeps its reason on the status after it exits, and a refusal after a slow start is reported.` ADR 0015 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): the daemon prints its refusal and exits, and the supervisor wrote "the daemon exited" over the refusal when stdout closed, so the panel kept the sentence that says nothing; a refusal after the 20 s ready budget was dropped. `wait_for_ready` reports every failure in order and `exit_error` keeps how the wait ended; two daemon.rs tests pin both.
- `fix(demo-kit): the athena block a WebMCP tool carries says internal where the kit says data, so the gate reads the page's own claim instead of discarding it as unknown.` README §3.3, design 5.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `useWebMCPTool` sent the kit's `side_effects: "data"`, a value `SIDE_EFFECTS` in `contracts/manifest.py` and `gate.js` do not have, so `flagsOf` dropped the block for every app-data tool and fell back to the hints (`inferred_from: annotations`); read alone, a reversible data write classified GATED. `athenaFor` maps the kit's word to the contract's (`wireSideEffects` in `webmcp/types.ts`), `ManifestTool.side_effects` now carries the contract's vocabulary, and the hooks docblock counts its five branches. `examples/demo-kit/test/ui-webmcp.test.ts` drives all six claims through `gate.js`.
- `fix(demo-kit): the template's navigate refuses a record id whose page is another view, instead of answering detail from the list or the log.` Template README, "Rules the app agents must not break" (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): `routeFor` answered ok for `..`, `.` and `activity`, whose pushed routes land on the list or the log, and `viewFor`'s `startsWith("/activity")` called a record page such as `/activity-log` the log. `viewFor` now lives beside `routeFor` in `lib/constants.ts`, and `routeFor` reads its own href back through it. Test: examples/demo-kit/test/demo-kit-template.test.ts.
- `fix(hands): a select the page disabled, or any control inside a disabled fieldset, is refused rather than operated or reported as pressed.` README §3.4 tier 2 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): page_select never checked `disabled`, and the other acting hands read only the property, so a control in a `<fieldset disabled>` was "clicked" though `click()` did nothing. One `inert` check (`disabled` or `:disabled`) now backs page_find's mark and page_click, page_fill and page_select; two hands.test.ts cases pin it.
- `fix(demo-kit): every object a WebMCP tool schema publishes is closed to unknown arguments, as the page's door already enforces.` README §3.4, design 5.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor; mcp-tools/tool-schema-design): `door.ts` refuses a key the schema does not name, at the top and in every nested object, while `parametersToJsonSchema` published no `additionalProperties`, which JSON Schema reads as open. The model and the core's validator were told an extra argument was fine and only the page said otherwise. `examples/demo-kit/test/ui-webmcp.test.ts` walks the schema and finds no open object.
- `fix(demo-kit): the template's comments name the kit's seam instead of listing and counting the sibling apps, and stop pointing at a README appendix that does not exist.` templates-scaffolding / template-portability (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): "three of the four reference apps (hirelane, ledgerbox, tidycrm)" was already false (atlas uses the same seam) and every copy is a fifth app reading "all four"; providers.tsx pointed at an appendix the README never had. Test: examples/demo-kit/test/demo-kit-template.test.ts, with the sibling names read from the tree.
- `fix(demo-kit): a DataTable sort control is named by the header a reader sees, and the sorted column says its order with aria-sort.` README §3.4 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): the sort button's `aria-label` was `Sort by <column key>`, so the template's "Updated" header was announced as "Sort by updated_at" (WCAG 2.5.3, label in name), and the order lived only in an aria-hidden arrow. `examples/demo-kit/test/ui-webmcp.test.ts` renders the table (the kit's `.tsx` through its own TypeScript) and checks the name against the visible header.
- `fix(hands): the screenshot refusal for a tab not on screen is one sentence again, not one with twenty-two spaces in the middle.` README §3.4 tier 2 (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): a `format!` literal broken across two lines without a `\` carried the source indentation into the refusal the model reads and a card shows. Now `not_on_screen`, pinned by a hands.rs test that forbids a run of spaces.
- `fix(demo-kit): the template's design spends the kit's spacing scale instead of re-typing --dk-gap as 12px, so re-pointing a step moves the whole app.` Kit stylesheet contract (scan-sweep stabilize, visual-craft): the template pinned `--dk-gap` as a literal and called it "the single spacing step every kit component reads", though the kit has a thirteen-step scale and `--dk-gap` is step 6; `.dk-level` spent `calc(var(--dk-gap) / 2)` where the scale has `--dk-space-3`. Rendered values unchanged. Test: examples/demo-kit/test/demo-kit-template.test.ts.
- `fix(demo-kit): withTx rolls back only a transaction that is still open, so the error that ended one is the error the caller sees.` ADR 0017 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): SQLite ends a transaction itself on a full disk, an I/O error or an interrupt, and `raw` is handed out for what the wrappers do not cover; the unconditional `ROLLBACK` in the catch then threw "cannot rollback - no transaction is active" in place of the real cause. It now checks `isTransaction` first. `examples/demo-kit/test/ui-webmcp.test.ts` ends a transaction under `withTx` and gets the original error back.
- `fix(bridge): closing a tab answers the calls still waiting on it at once, instead of after the relay's 35 s timer.` ADR 0101 (scan-sweep stabilize, state-coverage): a call in flight when its tab closed waited for a page that no longer existed, with the run stuck in "acting". `Bridge::drop_tab`, called from `tabs::close`, answers them with the timer's own `timeout` (so a write still reads as `outcome_unknown`) and forgets the tab's nonce and late-answer memory; a bridge.rs test pins it.
- `fix(demo-kit): openDb closes the connection a throwing seed opened, so a broken seed does not leak a handle per request or lock the file the READMEs say to delete.` ADR 0017 (scan-sweep stabilize, risk-assessor): the handle is memoised only after a successful seed, so every request re-opened the file and left the failed connection open; on Windows the data file then could not be deleted to reseed while the dev server ran. `examples/demo-kit/test/ui-webmcp.test.ts` seeds badly, then well, and removes the directory.
- `fix(demo-kit): the template drops the .dk-flight rule, whose element the echo replaced, so a copy no longer ships a completion signal that is never drawn.` (scan-sweep stabilize, code-optimizer): a8e5116 replaced the span with `<Echo>` and left the rule and its comment; zero consumers by `git grep`. Test: examples/demo-kit/test/demo-kit-template.test.ts, every class the template styles is drawn by the template or the kit.
- `fix(demo-kit): the template's L2 overlay is centred by its insets and auto margins, instead of starting half its width right of centre.` (scan-sweep stabilize, ui-perfectionist): `inset: 10% 50% auto` with a fixed width resolves to `left: 50%` in LTR, and `translate: 50% 0` pushed it further right; a 300px pane at 375px ended at 638px. Test: examples/demo-kit/test/demo-kit-template.test.ts, the box computed from the declarations at three widths, both directions.
- `fix(capture): at a half-pixel scale such as 112.5 % the card's picture is the page, not the whole window with the bar and strip.` README §5 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): the page rectangle's origin and size were rounded separately, so at 112.5 % (every one of 1,600 window heights probed) its bottom edge fell a pixel outside the window and the capture fell back to the whole window. Edges are now rounded once each, and a rectangle that still misses is refused rather than swapped for the chrome; a capture.rs test sweeps 11 scales.
- `fix(manifest): a page origin is read off its parsed host, so http://localhost.evil.example no longer passes the https-or-localhost rule, and the shell offers exactly what the daemon admits.` README §3.3, ADR 0119 (scan-sweep stabilize, security-auditor, assigned from contracts-seam): `HostManifest.validate` and the desktop's `registrable` both tested string prefixes, which admitted `http://localhost.evil.example`, `http://localhostile.net`, `https://` and `http://localhost:99999` and refused `http://127.0.0.1:3001`; `page_origin_admitted` now parses the origin and admits `https` with a host or `http` on `localhost` (`LOOPBACK_PAGE_HOSTS`, mirrored in `manifest.ts`); `127.0.0.1` stays refused, as before. One accept/refuse table in `tests/contracts/test_manifest.py`, which `apps/desktop/src/lib/manifest.test.ts` reads row for row, plus a parity test on the host list; 4 of 5 refuse rows were admitted before. No manifest in the tree is newly refused.
- `fix(demo-kit): a WebMCP handler that throws leaves its stack in the page console as well as the in-band error the agent reads.` README §3.4 (scan-sweep stabilize, observability-auditor; mcp-tools/server-composition): the door turns a handler fault into `{ok: false, error}` so one bad tool cannot break the bridge, and discarded the error object, so the page's developer had nothing in devtools to say which handler broke. A refused argument is the caller's mistake and is not logged. `examples/demo-kit/test/ui-webmcp.test.ts` spies on `console.error`.
- `fix(demo-kit): the template's overlay escapes the record id in the selector it hands focus back through, as the echo's selector in the same file already does.` Layered-UI formula §1 rule 5 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): an id with a quote or backslash made `returnFocusTo` throw in a frame callback after close, so focus was dropped. Test: examples/demo-kit/test/demo-kit-template.test.ts.
- `fix(demo-kit): the template's level rows take a pointer and a hover ground, as the kit's own clickable controls do, so they read as buttons.` (scan-sweep stabilize, ui-perfectionist): `.dk-level-row` buttons had neither while `.dk-nav-link` and `.dk-table-row` on the same page answer the pointer. Test: examples/demo-kit/test/demo-kit-template.test.ts.
- `fix(demo-kit): the kit's stylesheet drops the manifest drawer's eleven rules, which no element in the tree has ever drawn, and a test keeps every styled class drawn.` README §3.4 (scan-sweep stabilize, ui-perfectionist): `.dk-manifest*` shipped to every app importing `demo-kit.css` with a raw `160ms ease` transition and no reduced-motion path, and no file since the kit landed renders it (zero-consumer grep over examples/, apps/, packages/). `examples/demo-kit/test/ui-webmcp.test.ts` walks examples/ for every class the sheet styles, with the two kept scaffold classes named with reasons and checked both ways.
- `fix(daemon): a damaged or locked brain index fails on the failure line, so the shell shows the reconcile remedy instead of "exited without a ready line".` README §3.5, ADR 0011, ADR 0081 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): `serve` caught only `OSError` and `ValueError` while composing, so `IndexDamagedError` (a `sqlite3.DatabaseError`) or a locked index left a traceback on stderr and nothing on stdout; it now catches `sqlite3.Error` too and prints the brain's own sentence on the failure line. Test: `tests/daemon/test_ready.py` (a real `serve` over an index that is not a database), red before with an empty stdout.
- `fix(daemon): the daemon's port is its own on Windows, so a second daemon or a socket asking for reuse cannot bind beside it.` README §3.5, ADR 0011 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter / security-auditor): `DaemonServer` inherited `allow_reuse_address`, and on Windows `SO_REUSEADDR` lets a second socket bind a listening port, so a second `athena serve` printed a ready line beside the first and any process asking for reuse could receive the shell's token header; Windows now binds with `SO_EXCLUSIVEADDRUSE`, POSIX keeps `SO_REUSEADDR`. Tests in `tests/daemon/test_server.py`: a second daemon and a reuse-asking socket are both refused (red before: the second daemon bound), and a closed daemon's port rebinds at once.
- `fix(daemon): a decision naming too many switched-off origins is refused whole with a 400, as a run is, instead of a 500.` README §3.2 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `POST /run` caught `ListTooLong` from `disabled_origins_from` and `POST /decisions/<id>` did not, so the same over-cap list answered 500 `ListTooLong`; `decide` now refuses it as `validator_failed` before anything is touched. Test: `tests/daemon/test_decisions.py`, red before with a 500.
- `fix(daemon): a route or socket that raises leaves one line in the daemon's log, naming the route and the type and quoting nothing.` README §3.5, ADR 0011 (scan-sweep stabilize, observability-auditor): the dispatcher's 500 and the socket door's 1011 were the only trace of a route's bug, since stdout is the ready line's and the request log is off; both now write `[daemon] <method> <path> raised <type>` to stderr, which the shell pumps into its log, never the message or the query string. Test: `tests/daemon/test_server.py`, red before with nothing on stderr.
- `fix(daemon): the package and routes docstrings name every module and base route, and a test holds them to the table.` README §3.5 (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): `athena.daemon` said four files and listed four of six, called `/health` a write and named neither `/run/stop` nor `/engines`; `routes.py` counted five reads and omitted `GET /engines`. Two tests in `tests/daemon/test_routes.py` derive the expectation from `base_routes` and the package's files (red before: `GET /engines`; `connectors`, `voice`).
- `fix(desktop): a timed-out tabs_read is judged by the shell hand's flags, the tool the call runs, not by a page's tool of that name.` ADR 0105, ADR 0101 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `isReadCall` read a page's own `tabs_read` before the hand although `callOnPage` always runs the hand, so whether its timeout was `timeout` or `outcome_unknown` depended on flags for a tool that never runs; it now picks the list with `isHandCall`, the router's rule. `src/lib/execute.test.ts` pins both sides.
- `fix(hirelane): propose_slots holds time only for someone at Interview, three slots each in all, never three more per call.` DESIGN-LAW §7.7 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor; a policy-tighten): `proposeSlots` held three free slots for anyone at any stage, and three more on every call, while scheduling refuses everyone below Interview or decided, so those holds only locked the calendar. `lib/acts.ts` `proposeReason` and `slotsToHold` now decide it for the action and the dossier alike: Applied and Screening refuse as too early, Offer and Rejected as decided, three held refuses as `already_held`, fewer tops up to three; the dossier's Propose button names its reason. Tests: test/acts.test.ts (4 new cases, the drain loop rewritten), test/actions.test.ts.
- `fix(components): a value rail whose value matches no option still has its one tab stop.` README 3.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): PillGroup put tabIndex 0 only on the option equal to `value`, so the Whisper model picker (`value=""` before a model is chosen) had no tab stop and could not be reached from the keyboard, and a chosen option with a disabledReason left the stop on a button that cannot take focus. The stop is now the chosen option or the first that can be picked. Tests: components/PillGroup.test.

- `fix(companion): an answer whose store call fails or ends without a verdict gives the buttons back, instead of reading Sending for good.` README §3.1, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): the machine waits in `sending` until the store calls `settled`, and `answerSheet` could end or throw without calling it, so her slip read Sending... with both buttons off until a reload; the runtime now hears the store call's promise and settles one that ends without a verdict as a refusal that keeps the card. `src/companion/runtime.test.ts` pins three cases.
- `fix(hirelane): a person's sent letters stay on their card however many the app has sent since, instead of the newest fifty in the whole app.` (scan-sweep stabilize, state-coverage): `buildBoard` read `listMessages()`, which kept the newest 50 messages app-wide, so past fifty sends a rejected applicant's "Already sent" and the agent's `already_sent` read empty with nothing to say they were cut. The read is uncapped now (its only reader files every message under its person) and its uncalled per-applicant branch is gone. Test: test/board.test.ts.
- `fix(components): the value rail's arrows step over an option that cannot be picked instead of stalling on it.` README 3.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): PillGroup focused the next index even when it was a disabled button, which cannot take focus, so an arrow press next to an unavailable option picked nothing and every later press started from the same place; options past it were unreachable by arrow, and Home/End failed the same way. The walk now steps over unavailable options and wraps. Tests: components/PillGroup.test.
- `fix(components): putting the close confirm away gives focus back to the close button instead of dropping it on the document.` README 3.1, 3.5, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): Keep or Escape unmounted the focused button and mounted the close button with nothing focused, so the next Tab started from the top of the document. The close button now takes focus back when the confirm is put away, and a first render moves nothing. Tests: components/WindowControls.test (new).
- `fix(hirelane): read_view, open_group and set_filter see the groups the board draws under its filters, not every non-empty column.` (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): with "arguable only" on the board drew one row while the agent was told nine were there and could open the eight nobody could see; `boardGroups` in `components/board/tools/read.ts` reads the columns' own filter module, so the tools agree with the drawn rows in 6 of 6 filter combinations (was 1). Test: test/groups.test.ts.
- `fix(companion): an approve of a sheet with no row ticked is refused before it is sent, so the key or chord cannot decline every row under an Approved stamp.` ADR 0104 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): the sheet's Approve button is off at zero ticked rows, but the A key and the approve chord reached `answerSheet`, which declined every row and reported the press taken, so the slip stamped it APPROVED; `approveRunsNothing` (`companion/model.ts`) is now asked at the runtime's answer door in `live.tsx`. `src/companion/model.test.ts` pins it.
- `fix(components): the close confirm is described by its question, so a screen reader says why it asks.` README 3.1, ADR 0026 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): the alertdialog's question was a sibling span nothing pointed at, so with focus on Keep a reader announced "Confirm close" and "Keep" and never "A decision is waiting". The dialog's aria-describedby now names the question. Tests: components/WindowControls.test.
- `fix(hirelane): an act or an Undo whose server action throws ends in a toast saying the outcome is unknown, instead of taking the whole board down.` ADR 0101 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): `useRun` awaited every act and Undo bare inside a transition, and with no error boundary in the app a thrown action replaced the board with the framework's error page; `settle` turns the throw into an error toast that claims neither success nor failure and points at the History band. Test: test/run.test.ts.
- `fix(companion): what she says goes into the one live region she started with, so a screen reader voices it instead of meeting a region born with its text.` README §3.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): her `role="status"` region was keyed on the sentence counter, so each announcement (a decision waiting, sending, approved, a refusal) mounted a new region with its text already in it, which most readers do not voice; the region now stays and the words are keyed inside it. `src/companion/views.dom.test.tsx` pins both.
- `fix(proving): a provider or eval test runs only when -m names its marker, so a key in the environment never makes a bare pytest spend.` README §9 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist): CI and AGENTS.md run bare `uv run pytest`, and with `NEBIUS_API_KEY` set that ran `tests/proving/test_live.py` against Token Factory; `tests/proving/conftest.py` now skips a `provider` or `eval` test unless the run's `-m` names it (`uv run pytest -m provider -k live` still runs it). `tests/proving/test_live.py` runs pytest over a three-test tree to pin both ways.
- `fix(hirelane): the agent's dossier read says which of a person's slots is a booked interview and which is only held.` (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): `meta.held` and the agent's `slots_held` listed a booked, emailed interview exactly like a hold nobody had sent, so an agent deciding whether to write had nothing saying a letter had gone; every entry now carries `status: "held" | "booked"` (additive). Test: test/acts.test.ts.
- `fix(styles): --bar-height and --strip-height are held to layout.rs by a test, not by a sentence in two files.` README 3.5, ADR 0088 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): app.css and layout.rs each said the other spells the same two heights and nothing checked it, while the shape ids they share already had a parity test. Seeded red at 41px. Tests: styles/layout-parity.test (new).
- `fix(hirelane): the domain lib and board model drop eight exports nothing reads, and a test counts readers for every export in them.` (scan-sweep stabilize, code-optimizer): `diff`/`BdDiff`/`scoreFor` (a head-to-head no component draws, which the board brief still credited and which scored the unscored as 0), `BdColumnGroup`, a second score-label function beside the one the dossier prints, and three row types nothing builds; the brief's Comparison row now names `criterionMedians()`. `APP_VERSION` is kept, named in the test, pending the studio-wide `athena:app` call. Test: test/exports.test.ts.
- `fix(proving): a lone surrogate in model text is written as its escape, so a paid run never loses its report, a ledger row or a judge call to one character.` README §9 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): a judge reason holding `json.loads`'s half-emoji (`\ud83d`) made `write_report` raise UnicodeEncodeError after the run's spend, leaving a cut `report.json` and no `report.md`; the report, `RunLog` and the Claude control's stdin and system file now encode with `report.TEXT_ERRORS` (backslashreplace), which the JSON reads back as the same string. Tests: `tests/proving/test_roles_and_budget.py`.
- `fix(components): the catalog is checked against the folder, and the push-to-talk pill nobody imports is gone.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, registry-conformance: ui-controls control-inventory-and-discovery): PushToTalk carried a @catalog tag, had no CATALOG.md row and no importer since Athena became her own window (push-to-talk is the shell's held chord, ADR 0027), so the component, its test, 85 lines of `.ptt` CSS and the motion test's `.ptt` pins are removed. Tests: components/catalog.test (new) holds every primitive to a tag, a row and an importer.
- `fix(hirelane): search_candidates' promises to an agent are pinned by a test: a score filter never matches the unscored, an employer matches by domain, an unknown role is refused.` (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist): `searchBoard` had no test, so the design law's "an unscored application is never a zero" had no instrument on the one tool that filters by score; seeded red by removing the scored guard. Test: test/search.test.ts.
- `fix(companion): a person who chose Calm sees her still, as the OS's reduced-motion setting already makes her; her sheet had no Calm rule at all.` ADR 0089 (scan-sweep stabilize, ui-perfectionist): `applyMotion` writes `data-motion` on her window's root, but `companion.css` read only `prefers-reduced-motion`, so Calm on a machine that does not ask for less motion left every loop running; each reduced-motion block now has a `:root[data-motion="calm"]` twin. `src/companion/companion.css.test.ts` holds every reduced-motion selector to its Calm twin.
- `fix(hirelane): three comments in the domain lib and the board tools stop misstating the code beside them.` (scan-sweep stabilize, documentation-auditor): the seed's ordering note cited a `listStageEvents` that no longer exists, BoardTools' header counted two tools over a file that registers four, and groups.ts carried its header twice. Comments only.
- `fix(proving): an answer whose brackets nest past the decoder's depth is a counted not_json, not a RecursionError that ends the run.` README §9 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): `extract_json` caught only JSONDecodeError, so a degenerate `[[[[` repetition raised RecursionError past `call_json`'s `except ValueError` with no ledger row or count and out of the run's worker; it is now no value at that start and the run of openers is stepped over (60,000 brackets in 0.009 s). Test: `tests/proving/test_roles_and_budget.py`.
- `fix(styles): app.css drops the rules, keyframes and tokens no view uses, and a test holds every rule to a user.` README 3.1, ADR 0095 (scan-sweep stabilize, tech-debt-tracker): `.split`/`.split--letter` and their media query, `.harness`, `.harness__body`, `.truncate`, `@keyframes layer-sheet`/`layer-scrim` and the six `--page-*` tokens in both themes had no reader anywhere in the repo; 75 lines removed. Tests: styles/sheet-users.test (new): every class is spelled by a view, every keyframes animated, every custom property read.
- `fix(companion): A, D and Escape still answer with a sheet row's tick focused; only a box that takes text counts as typing.` ADR 0104 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): her keyboard rule counted every `INPUT` as typing, and the only inputs in her window are the sheet's checkboxes, so a keyboard user who unticked a row could not then press A or D; `typingIn` (`companion/runtime.ts`) now counts only a box that takes text. `src/companion/runtime.test.ts` pins it.
- `fix(styles): the lazy-depth placeholder guard reads which depths are lazy from the tree, not from a list of five names.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist): source-guards held five depth names by hand, so a sixth lazy depth could ship `fallback: null` with the guard green; it now checks every scenes/*/live.tsx that calls lazy() and still requires the five. Seeded red with a scratch sixth depth. Tests: styles/source-guards.test.
- `fix(companion): a step on her tape, in her Record and in the tray line names its app as the card does, not by the catalog id a page with no app name is filed under.` ADR 0065, ADR 0091 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `stepOf` read the app straight off `host.<app>.<tool>`, so a page with no published app showed `web_https_sledger_dtest` on her tape, in the tray line and in the Record's call rows beside decision rows saying `ledger.test`; it now names the app through `appOf`. `src/companion/model.test.ts` pins it.
- `fix(proving): every non-provider test under tests/proving is refused the network beyond loopback and a model CLI spawn, instead of trusting each test to fake.` README §9 (scan-sweep stabilize, security-auditor): the roles and the world default to the real Token Factory post, the real `claude` runner and the real engines, so a test that forgot one fake could spend; the autouse `offline_lane` fixture in `tests/proving/conftest.py` refuses a non-loopback lookup or connect and a `claude`/`codex` spawn (subprocess or asyncio) with an OSError, and leaves `provider`/`eval` tests alone. Tests: `tests/proving/test_live.py`.
- `fix(hirelane): a propose_slots top-up that holds one slot says "1 slot held", in the toast and in the History band.` (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): a top-up to three made one- and two-slot holds ordinary, and both the toast and the activity summary typed a plural. Test: test/acts.test.ts.
- `fix(styles): app.css no longer calls itself the whole stylesheet, and the catalog no longer names the deleted module bar.` README 3.1, ADR 0095 (scan-sweep stabilize, documentation-auditor): app.css's header claimed to be the app's whole stylesheet beside 18 other sheets (and the halo window does not load it), and CATALOG.md said WindowControls was used by the module bar; both now say what is true. Copy only.
- `fix(proving): a Nemotron answer cut at the role's own token ceiling is a counted void, never the model's bad JSON.` README §9 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `NebiusRole` ignored the engine's `finish_reason`, so an answer `max_tokens` cut mid-JSON was counted `not_json`/`bad_shape` against Nemotron; a `length` stop is now an engine_error naming the ceiling, `RoleReply.stopped`, a `cut` validity count and a `stopped` ledger field, with its tokens and cost kept. Test: `tests/proving/test_roles_and_budget.py`.
- `fix(styles): the close confirm's buttons take the interactive radius token instead of a one-off 4px, and the sheet's corners are held to the tokens.` README 3.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, ui-perfectionist): `.close-confirm__btn` was the one corner in app.css off the radius tokens, two pixels squarer than every other button; it now spends --radius-interactive. Tests: styles/motion-tokens.test (every border-radius is a --radius-* token, 999px or 0).
- `fix(companion): each line of her Record's model calls keeps its own id, read from the daemon's integer row_id, instead of sharing its turn's id or an empty one.` README §2 invariant 6 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): `GET /ledger` sends `row_id` as an integer and `ledgerFrom` read it as a string, so every line fell back to `turn_id` (shared by a turn's rounds) or `""`, and the list's React keys collided; the old test pinned the defect with a string id the daemon never sends. `src/companion/model.test.ts` now feeds the wire shape.
- `fix(proving): a world whose manifest is refused closes its brain and removes its throwaway root before the error leaves.` README §9 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): `World.__init__` raised `WorldError` after opening the brain over a temporary root, so no `with` and no `close()` ever ran and each refused manifest leaked an open brain and an `athena-world-*` directory; it now closes what it opened before re-raising, and `close()` removes the root even if the brain's close raises. Test: `tests/proving/test_world_and_verdict.py`.
- `fix(companion): while the next card's capture is read, the slip says it is reading instead of showing the last card's picture under the new card's id.` ADR 0066 (scan-sweep stabilize, state-coverage): the slip's paper is not remounted when a card leaves unstamped, and `ShotFigure` kept its last read across a new id, so the next card showed the previous page captioned with its own id; a read is now kept with the id it was for. `src/companion/views.dom.test.tsx` pins it.
- `fix(proving): the Haiku control's claude child no longer inherits NEBIUS_API_KEY, which every proving run holds.` README §9 (scan-sweep stabilize, security-auditor): `main` refuses to start without the Nemotron key and `subprocess_runner` passed the whole environment minus the CLI billing switches, so the control that reads attacker-written fixtures held a key it never uses; `control_env` drops it for that child only (`claude_env` keeps it for the hosted runner's Python child, and now says so). Test: `tests/proving/test_roles_and_budget.py`.
- `fix(companion): a card no longer waits forever on her window saying whether it is on screen; after 500 ms the page's own knowledge decides, and later cards are not queued behind it.` ADR 0026 (scan-sweep stabilize, risk-assessor): card changes are applied in order, and an arrival awaited `isVisible()` with no deadline, so one IPC call that never settled held that card and every later one for the window's life; the question now races `VISIBLE_MS` on the runtime's clock. `src/companion/runtime.test.ts` pins it.
- `fix(companion): the Record's `.rec-waiting` style and the fixtures' STATE_FIXTURES list go: nothing produces the one or reads the other.` README §3.5 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist): `STATE_FIXTURES` had no reader (`git grep` finds its declaration only) and no Record row's result is ever "waiting"; both are removed and the stylesheet's dead-class guard names `.rec-waiting`.
- `fix(proving): the purse states its real overshoot - one call per worker in flight, not one call - and a test pins it.` ADR 0030 (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): `budget.py` promised an overshoot of "at most the one call" in flight, but the purse is checked before and charged after each call and shared by `--workers` threads, so four calls held past the check all spent; the docstring now says one per worker, and `tests/proving/test_roles_and_budget.py` pins four calls and the refusal after.
- `fix(tidycrm): a merge that carries a newer activity date over restamps the survivor's stale flag.` Design 4.6.4 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `mergeContacts` adopted the newer `last_activity_at` but left the stored `is_stale` at its seed value, so 3 of the seed's 60 pairs left a survivor active last month in the stale segment, where the block's delete-stale gate offers it; `isStaleAt` in `lib/db.ts` is now the one cutoff rule for the seed and the merge, pinned by `test/domain-lib.test.ts`.
- `fix(harness): no CLI child inherits NEBIUS_API_KEY any more; the Proving Ground's run child, the one spawn that calls Nemotron, is handed it by name.` ADR 0007 (scan-sweep stabilize, security-auditor, agent-cli-transport/subscription-auth-selection): `ENGINE_KEYS["nebius"]` named nothing because `proving/server/runner.py` built its child's environment through the shared door, so every claude and codex child the daemon spawned held the Token Factory key; the door now strips it and the runner adds it back for its own child only. Tests: harness/test_subprocess_exit (a real child sees no `NEBIUS_API_KEY`, red before), proving/test_server (the run child gets the key and no CLI key; red with the door change alone), proving/test_roles_and_budget (the door pin strengthened).
- `fix(tidycrm): a record merged away no longer counts as a company spelling, so settling its domain clears the conflict.` Design 4.6.4 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): the spelling counts and `refreshConflict` read every undeleted row while `resolve_company` rewrites only live, unmerged ones, so merging a duplicate and then settling the name left the domain spelled two ways forever (16 of 60 seed pairs sit on conflicted domains); one `WORKING` predicate now feeds all four, the merge restamps its domain, and `test/domain-lib.test.ts` pins it.
- `fix(ledgerbox): a period's Collected counts every payment against the invoices it describes, so Invoiced = Collected + Outstanding on every month.` Design 4.6.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): `summarize` also required a payment's own date to fall in the period, so a June invoice paid in July left June's Collected and joined no other month's; June's close printed $637,652 that was in none of its three figures and July $424,682. `test/ledgerbox-domain-lib.test.ts` pins the identity on all four periods.
- `fix(perf): the Chores perf drive presses "Talk it through with her", the control the topic page renders where "Fit it to me" was, and a test holds every name it presses to the scene.` README §3.1, ADR 0089 and 0097 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist, quality-gates/renameable-detector-keys): `perf:all` stopped at `perf:chores` after 30 s waiting for a button c0cb495 removed; the drive now opens the Adopt studio from the topic and closes it, and `scripts/perf-chores.drive.test.ts` renders each level from the drive's own fixture and fails on a name or selector the scene no longer draws. `perf:all` passes all eight scenes.
- `fix(tidycrm): undo refuses, with the reason, to replay a write over a newer one or to reopen a pair whose record is gone.` Design 4.6.4 (scan-sweep stabilize, risk-assessor): entries can be undone in any order and `applyUndo` replayed them blind, so undoing a tidy after a `resolve_company` overwrote the settled name and undoing a skip after a delete reopened a pair nobody could act on (pairs_open 59 against read_pairs 58); the strategies now check the field still holds what they wrote and both records are still listed, and `undoWrite` turns the refusal into the tool's reason, pinned by `test/domain-lib.test.ts`.
- `fix(harness): the live Nemotron test under tests/harness runs only when -m names provider, so a key in the environment never makes a bare pytest spend.` README §9 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist, test-harness/isolation-lanes): dd054fb's opt-in hook lived in `tests/proving/conftest.py` and bound only that directory, so `test_live_one_turn_against_nemotron` still made two Token Factory calls from a bare run with `NEBIUS_API_KEY` set; `tests/harness/conftest.py` now carries the same hook. Tests: harness/test_tokenfactory runs pytest over a three-test tree with a key set (red before: 3 passed; now 1 passed, 2 skipped).
- `fix(ledgerbox): mark_paid's amount reading refuses a misplaced separator or a fraction of a cent instead of reading it a hundred or a thousand times off.` Design 5.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): `parseDollars` stripped every comma, apostrophe and space as noise, so `1,25` recorded $125.00 and `1.250,00` $1.25 on a PERMANENT act, and sub-cent amounts were rounded; grouping marks now count only between groups of three, two decimals at most, and a number off a whole cent is refused. `test/ledgerbox-domain-lib.test.ts` pins both sides.
- `fix(tidycrm): the CSV export opens a formula-shaped cell as text, and leaves signed numbers and phones alone.` Design 4.6.4 (scan-sweep stabilize, security-auditor, export-escapes-for-its-consumer): `resolve_company` accepts any name an agent gives it and `lib/csv.ts` only quoted, so `=HYPERLINK(...)` reached the exported file as a live formula; the encoder now guards then quotes, exempting signed numbers and phone shapes, and `test/domain-lib.test.ts` pins six hostile cells guarded and all 800 seeded rows byte-identical.
- `fix(ledgerbox): a reminder letter says how many days past due the invoice is, never a send date the books do not hold, and says nothing late about one still inside terms.` Design 4.6.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): the gentle opening said an invoice "went out N days ago" with N = days past due (a letter for an invoice issued 91 days before the close said 70), and both tones floored the count at one, so an invoice inside terms was "1 days past its due date". `test/ledgerbox-domain-lib.test.ts` pins both tones at 0, 1 and 21 days.
- `fix(ledgerbox): the lanes leave the draft out of invoiced and still-open, as the totals beside them do.` Design 4.6.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `buildSheet` skipped only `void` where `summarize` skips `COUNTED_OUT`, so the seed's $73,100 draft sat in the Unfiled lane's figures and in no total, and the lanes summed $73,100 over the mast's Outstanding and Invoiced. `test/ledgerbox-domain-lib.test.ts` pins the three lane sums to their totals.
- `fix(tidycrm): the mutation door checks at run time what its types only promise - verdicts, rule names, ids and one-line company names.` Design 5.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, import-validation): the server actions are HTTP endpoints and `lib/mutations.ts` trusted their TypeScript types, so `resolvePair(pair, "merged")` closed a pair as merged with no merge, unknown rule names reached the activity log, and a company name with a line break was written to a whole domain; the library now refuses all three, pinned by `test/domain-lib.test.ts`.
- `fix(ledgerbox): no credit is offered for an invoice it is larger than, since applying it is refused.` Design 4.6.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `scoreCandidate` scored lines up to 5% over the balance ("over by $10.00, about a wire fee") that `matchAction` always refuses, so a card could offer a match that could only fail; the scorer now drops them first. `test/ledgerbox-domain-lib.test.ts` pins it.
- `fix(tidycrm): the export's Markdown table prints a company name as one cell of text, whatever punctuation it carries.` Design 4.6.4 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, export-escapes-for-its-consumer): the CSV half of the export was guarded and the Markdown half interpolated names raw, so a `|` split the row and `[..](..)` arrived as a live link on the page the summary is appended to; cells now escape table and inline punctuation, a no-op on every seeded name, pinned by `test/domain-lib.test.ts`.
- `fix(ledgerbox): the invoice card offers a draft no credit, as its row and the strip already did.` Design 4.6.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `getInvoiceDetail` asked only whether a balance was left, so a credit fitting the draft was on its card and in `read_invoice` while its row and the strip said none; one `takesCredit` predicate on `COUNTED_OUT` now answers for all three. `test/ledgerbox-domain-lib.test.ts` pins it.
- `fix(chores): an open lightbox keeps Tab on its own controls, and an Escape that arrives from outside it closes the still instead of leaving the page.` README §3.5, ADR 0096 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker, modal-stack): in Edge, the fourth Tab left the `aria-modal` dialog for the stage dock, and Escape then rose to the catalogue with the still still open, losing the reader's place in the gallery; Tab now wraps in `lightbox.tsx` and the topic's Escape closes an open still first. Tests: wired.test.tsx.
- `fix(tidycrm): the export page says its cleanup figures are the whole list's, not the segment's it just named.` Design 4.6.4 (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): the summary opened "N contacts in **Client contacts**, after this cleanup:" over figures counted across every revision and pair, so a non-client tidy moved the clients page's count; the sentence now states the scope, pinned by `test/domain-lib.test.ts`.
- `fix(ledgerbox): one day past due and one overdue invoice are said in the singular, on the sheet and in the close.` Design 4.6.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): the hover read-out said "1 days past due." on two seeded invoices and both export forms said "across 1 invoices"; the export's two renderings now share one `overdueLine`. `test/ledgerbox-domain-lib.test.ts` pins both.
- `fix(chores): the topic page's figure tiles and flow steps pad on the density tokens, as the replay's number tiles and card tiles already do.` README §3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, ui-perfectionist, parity-auditor): 1657861 moved the `ch-` tiles off 14px but its guard covered only the `ch-` half of the sheet, and the catalogue rewrite brought `.pa-kpi` (14 16) and `.pa-step` (14 14 12) back; they read `--density-pad-sm` / `--density-pad` now and `chores.css.test.ts` holds every non-control rule of the `pa-` half to it.
- `fix(tidycrm): a write on one record answers in the singular, as its activity line already did.` Design 4.6.4 (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): the answers the toast shows and the agent reads back said "Normalised 1 fields across 1 contacts.", "Flagged 1 contacts", "Deleted 1 contacts" and "Filed 1 contacts" beside log lines that pluralised correctly; one `count()` helper spells all four, pinned by `test/domain-lib.test.ts`.
- `fix(proving): a cancelled run whose report cannot be written still ends, so the trigger page's runner is not left busy until a restart.` ADR 0039 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): `Runner._read` wrote the cancelled report unguarded, so an `OSError` killed the reader before its `end` and `done`, and every later start answered 409 until the server restarted; the write is guarded and the run ends saying its report could not be written (its spend still counts, from the ledger). Tests: proving/test_server (red before: still busy 20 s after the cancel).
- `fix(tidycrm): read_conflicts on a domain nobody in the list is on answers with nothing, not an entry with an empty consensus.` Design 4.6.4 (scan-sweep stabilize, state-coverage): an unknown domain came back as "(showing 1 of 1)" with `consensus: ""` and zero contacts, the same shape as a real one; it now answers with no entry, pinned by `test/domain-lib.test.ts`.
- `fix(ledgerbox): every act's tool class is checked against the class the act gives itself - its activity row and whether undo reverses it.` Design 5.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist): `lib/tool-classes.ts` was only ever compared with hand-kept copies of itself; `test/ledgerbox-domain-lib.test.ts` now drives all eight writes through their actions, reads each activity row, calls `undoAction`, and fails on any act whose reversibility differs from its declared class, with its own negative control.
- `fix(tidycrm): the page declares its WebMCP version from APP_VERSION instead of a second hand-typed "0.1.0".` Design 5.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `APP_VERSION` had no reader while `app/layout.tsx` typed the same literal into `athena:app-version`; the layout reads the constant and `test/domain-lib.test.ts` pins it and its match with package.json.
- `fix(proving): HEAD on a run's event stream answers headers only, instead of writing every frame and holding a thread for the run's length.` ADR 0037 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): `do_HEAD` routes through `do_GET` and only `_send_text` honoured HEAD, so `HEAD /runs/<id>/events` streamed the whole run as a body; `_stream` now stops after its headers. Tests: proving/test_server (bare-socket HEAD; red before: frames in the body).
- `fix(tidycrm): the root layout's note no longer sends a reader to a deleted per-route layout.` Design 4.6.4 (scan-sweep stabilize, documentation-auditor): `app/layout.tsx` described faces in `app/v/blocks/layout.tsx` and swappable directions, both gone; it now names `app/page.tsx` and `components/blocks/Blocks.tsx`, where they live.
- `fix(ledgerbox): the domain lib drops ten exports nothing reads, and a test counts readers for every export in it.` (scan-sweep stabilize, code-optimizer): `FILTER_LABEL`, `listBankLines`, `matchIndex`, `getClient`, `listExports`, `countByFilter`, `formatMonth`, `daysBetween`, `periodKey` and `ExportRecord` had no reader outside their own declaration (`git grep -lw`); they go, with the helpers only they used, and `test/ledgerbox-domain-lib.test.ts` counts readers for every export in the context's 18 files (`APP_VERSION` kept on purpose, as in hirelane).
- `fix(proving): the trigger page bounds its open event streams, so strangers cannot hold a server thread each for a live run's whole length.` ADR 0037 (scan-sweep stabilize, security-auditor): a run's event stream is public and holds its worker thread until the run ends, with no bound; past `MAX_STREAMS` (64) a stream now answers 503 `too_many_streams`, and every stream frees its slot when it ends. Tests: proving/test_server (cap 1: red before, a second stream answered 200).
- `fix(ledgerbox): an invoice 45+ days late says so first, under the glyph that already does, and still names the credit that might settle it.` Design 4.6.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): the card's standing glyph ranks 45+ days late above a credit, the sentence beside it (`mark.status`, also `read_view`'s `waiting_on`) ranked the credit first, so three seeded invoices 51-67 days late read "1 unapplied credit could belong here." under a "45+ days late" glyph. `test/ledgerbox-domain-lib.test.ts` pins the shared order.
- `fix(ledgerbox): the domain lib's comments name the readers that exist, not StripTools, EdgeHome, Chase.tsx, aging.ts and a reports or statement view.` (scan-sweep stabilize, documentation-auditor): eight present-tense references in `lib/db.ts`, `lib/export.ts` and `lib/filter-period.ts` pointed at surfaces no longer in the tree, and one line anchor into `app/actions.ts` had drifted; each now names its live reader. Comments only.
- `fix(proving): a Sandboxes reply with no uuid, a stream that is not base64 or a marker that is not UTF-8 is a probe's "no", not a crash of the whole spike.` ADR 0033 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): the probes catch `SandboxError` and `OSError`, but `reply["uuid"]`, `b64decode` and a strict `.decode()` raised `KeyError`, `binascii.Error` and `UnicodeDecodeError` past them, so one malformed reply lost every answer and the report; each now becomes the probe's evidence. Tests: proving/sandbox/test_sandbox_spike (three cases, each red before).
- `fix(perf): the Apps, Mind and Chores drives fail when a control they step through is gone, instead of skipping the step and passing a shorter path.` README §3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist, quality-gates/vacuous-by-evaluation): `if (tool)`, `if (choice)` and `if (await frame.count())` let a renamed tool, look or still drop its step in silence, so the gate judged less than its title names; each is a required click now, and `scripts/perf-drives.test.ts` runs every drive against a stand-in page missing that control and expects a failure.
- `fix(proving): every text colour on the trigger page reads at AA contrast in the light theme too; the live feed's lines were 2.97:1.` WCAG 2.2 SC 1.4.3 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): five rules painted small text in `--accent` (2.97 to 3.49:1 on the light surfaces) and `--ok` on the feed was 4.42:1; they take the until-now unused `--primary` and a darker light `--ok`. Tests: proving/test_server derives every `color:` rule from the page's CSS and checks it in both themes (red before: 18 pairs).
- `fix(proving): the trigger page's verdict strip is a labelled group, so its attack buttons keep their role for a screen reader.` WCAG 2.2 SC 4.1.2 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): the strip of per-attack buttons was `role="img"`, whose children are presentational, so its buttons were focusable with no announced role; it is now `role="group"` with the same label. Tests: proving/test_server (red before: role img).
- `fix(proving): the trigger page's theme button is named for what it shows ("Colour theme: Light"), not a fixed "Switch colour theme".` WCAG 2.2 SC 2.5.3 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): the button showed Auto, Light or Dark under a fixed aria-label that held none of those words; `applyTheme` now names it with the visible word and the current state. Tests: proving/test_server (red before: fixed label).
- `fix(proving): no text on the trigger page is set under 12px; four uppercase labels were 11px and mono ids about 11.2px.` The repo's 12px UI floor (scan-sweep stabilize, visual-craft): the fact labels, the history header, the fence label and the turn speaker were 11px and `.mono` computed to about 11.2px in the history; they are 12px and `max(12px, 0.86em)`. Tests: proving/test_server (red before: four 11px sizes).
- `fix(perf): perf:all judges every scene from one build and a red scene no longer hides the scenes after it.` README §3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist, parity-auditor): `perf:all` was eight `&&`-chained `perf:<scene>` runs, each with its own `vite build`, so the chores failure left mind unmeasured and a throwing drive printed only a stack; `perf-gate.mjs --all` now takes its scenes from the drives on disk, runs them all against one build, and reports a drive that did not finish as that scene's FAIL. `scripts/perf-gate.test.ts` pins the runner and the script/drive parity. 96 s to 68 s on this machine.
- `fix(chores): the catalogue's filters and pick, the front door's words and a topic's scroll outlive the fresh view the stage mounts at every move.` README §3.5, ADR 0090, 0096 and 0098 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, bounty-hunter): each move mounts a new layer, so `useState` in ChoresView was gone on every return; in Edge a Recover money / Most value catalogue came back as All jobs / Best edge with no row picked, a topic left at 2229 px came back at 0, and the need said at the front door could not reach the studio it opened. They live in `chores/memory.ts` now, as the replay's playing flag does; wired.test.tsx and intake.test.tsx remount the view at every move as the stage does.
- `fix(accounts): Enter in a connect field is refused exactly where the button is, so no second connect or consent page goes out while one is in flight.` README 4, ADR 0117 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter; async-ui-states/action-busy-states): the token's and the client secret's Enter called the send the disarmed button guards, so a consent page open meant a second flow on Enter. `interact.test.tsx` pins both, seeded red then green.
- `fix(apps): the page band's tools pill says "1 tool" as the field does, not "1 tools" with a "tool(s)" title.` README 3.4, ADR 0088 (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): `appOf` pluralised the count and the strip did not. New `strip.test.tsx`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(scenes): the Accounts and Apps text fields let go on Escape as the allow-list's does, so Escape can rise out of a depth from a field.` README 3.5, the stage's Escape rule (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): the register box, the page band's address field and the three connect fields held Escape, and the stage never takes a typing target's. Three interaction tests, seeded red then green.
