- `fix(stores): Clear in the middle of a turn leaves the turn running and stoppable, and never drops a page result waiting for the next message.` README 3.2, ADR 0115 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, state-coverage; client-state/status-fsms): `clear()` spread `EMPTY`, so a Clear while a request streamed set the phase idle (Stop gone, composer open) and emptied `outstanding`, losing a write Stop had kept for the next message. Clear now empties the conversation only. `run.test.ts` "Clear", seeded red then green.
- `fix(proving): a Gauntlet run against a gate that waves everything through fails proof 1, keeps each breach and prints it; no run test had ever seen a breach.` README §9, ADR 0032 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist; eval-harness): every `run_gauntlet` test ended held, so a proof that always passes and a `_capture` that never runs both survived the suite (mutation probe). New test_gauntlet run with a sabotaged gate, seeded red against both mutants.
- `fix(stores): a continuation the daemon refused, or that Stop caught before it left, keeps the page results it carried for the next message.` README 3.2, ADR 0115 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler, bounty-hunter; hitl-approval/resume-after-decision): `consume()` emptied `outstanding` into the request before the capture and the POST, so a pre-stream refusal or a Stop during the continuation's screenshot lost a write that had run on the page. Those rows now go back for the next message, and the aborted request is not sent. `run.test.ts` "a continuation that never reached the daemon", seeded red then green.
- `fix(hirelane): every server action lands its change and its activity row together, so a rejection never stands on file with no row saying it was sent.` DESIGN-LAW §7, AGENTS.md "Ledger everything" (scan-sweep stabilize, risk-assessor, observability-auditor): the seven actions wrote the change and the row as separate autocommits, so a failed row left an unrecorded rejection, offer or hold. Each now runs in one `withTx`, as ledgerbox's do. `test/atomic.test.ts` fails the row for all seven acts, seeded red (8 of 8) then green.
- `fix(accounts): the allow-list knows an entry is already listed by its lower case, as the vault keeps it, instead of sending a capitalised copy that changes nothing.` README 4, ADR 0062 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): the draft compared exact strings while `vault.set_allowlist` lower-cases every entry. `interact.test.tsx` pins it, seeded red then green.
- `fix(proving): a breach whose world cannot be copied out is still a breach; the failed copy is reported beside it, not turned into an error that proof 1 passes over.` README §9, ADR 0032 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler, observability-auditor): `_capture` ran inside the driver's catch-all, so a locked brain file or a full disk made a breached turn `error` and proof 1 read PASS. Tests: test_gauntlet (red before: proof 1 passed with 2 breaches as errors).
- `fix(hirelane): undoing an older move after a newer one leaves the newer move standing and says so, instead of silently reversing both.` DESIGN-LAW §7 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, parity-auditor): the History band offers Undo on every row, and an older move's undo restored its FROM stage over a later move, unlogged. It now restores only when its move is still the newest and otherwise logs that it left the stage alone. Three `actions.test.ts` cases (the decision guard had none), seeded red then green.
- `fix(accounts): removing an allow-list entry no longer drops the keyboard's place; focus goes to the Remove now in that place, or the draft once the list is empty.` WCAG 2.2 SC 2.4.3 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): Disconnect and Forget kept focus, Remove did not. `interact.test.tsx` pins three removals, seeded red then green.
- `fix(hirelane): the design-law check fails a negative px length as it fails a positive one, and a test now watches each of its rules go red.` DESIGN-LAW §9.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, test-strategist; quality-gates): the `-` that kept the match out of `--name-12px` also hid every negative length, so `margin-block-start: -12px` passed `lint`. `check-law.mjs` takes a fixture root; `test/law.test.ts` seeds each violation in a temp tree, red (2 of 4 lengths caught) then green.
- `fix(stores): a card answered while its turn still streams rides that turn's continuation instead of starting a second turn beside it, and no longer replaces the turn's snapshot.` README 3.2, ADR 0067, 0104 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, parity-auditor; client-state/async-race-guards): `answer()` started `exchange(CONTINUE)` while the filing turn was still in flight (two `POST /run` for one conversation) and `answerOne()` overwrote the exchange's snapshot, so the turn's own released calls were routed by the answer's later read. Exchanges are counted and the answer's result rides the running one; `onPage()` takes its snapshot. `run.test.ts` "an answer while a turn is still streaming", seeded red then green.
- `fix(proving): the approve-path probe holds an approval to its action and parameters by the grant's own refusal, so a gate that binds nothing no longer reads ok because the approval was already spent.` README §9, ADR 0036, 0038 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist, bounty-hunter; hitl-approval): the altered and swapped asks run after the first approval spent it, so with `ApprovalGrant.matches` answering yes to anything they were still refused, as `approval_spent`, and the probe said ok. They must now be refused as `validator_failed`. Tests: test_gauntlet (red before: ok on a grant bound to nothing).
- `fix(apps): closing a page from its chip no longer drops the keyboard's place; focus goes to the close now in that place, or the register box once none is open.` WCAG 2.2 SC 2.4.3 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): the chip unmounted with the focused close. `interact.test.tsx` closes every page in turn, seeded red then green.
- `fix(hirelane): the colour lock's check reads every CSS colour notation, and an exempt black or white stop no longer waves through the hue beside it.` DESIGN-LAW §4.2 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, bounty-hunter; quality-gates): `check-law.mjs` saw hex and `rgb()` only, and exempted whole lines that named `box-shadow`/`mask-image` or held one white plane. It now reads hsl/hwb/lab/lch/oklab/oklch/color() and strips only the exempt literals. `test/law.test.ts`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(hirelane): the colour lock's check reads the direction's TypeScript, so a hue typed into an inline style or an SVG fill fails lint like one in a stylesheet.` DESIGN-LAW §4.2 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, visual-craft; quality-gates): `check-law.mjs` opened `.ts`/`.tsx` for motion values only. Its script pass now runs the colour read too. `test/law.test.ts` seeds a `style={{ color }}` and an SVG `fill`, red then green.
- `fix(accounts): the consent ring breathes in and out instead of snapping back from its widest frame every 1.6s.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep stabilize, ui-perfectionist): the loop had no `alternate`. `view.test.tsx` pins it, seeded red then green; `perf:accounts` passes.
- `fix(proving): every Gauntlet detector now has a planted bug that makes it speak: an approval table that answers its own card, a refused gated executor that ran anyway, and a route that hands the page other parameters than the card.` README §9, ADR 0032, 0036 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist; eval-harness): deleting any of those three rules left the suite green (mutation probe). Tests: test_gauntlet, each seeded red against its mutant.
- `fix(hirelane): the contrast check fails a new ink rung or hue that has no floor, as its header always said it did.` DESIGN-LAW §9 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, accessibility-checker; quality-gates): `check-contrast.mjs` scored only the tokens listed in FLOORS, so an unlisted `--bd-ink-5` at about 2:1 passed `lint`. Every ink-ladder and locked-hue token in the block now needs a floor. `test/contrast-gate.test.ts` seeds one in a copy of the real block, red then green.
- `fix(stores): a turn that ends in turn.error stays in the error phase, as a refused request does, instead of reading "ready" under its own error.` README 3.2 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, state-coverage; client-state/status-fsms): `apply()` set the phase to error and `exchange()` then set it idle, so the panel said "ready" beneath the failure. `consume()` returns false after a `turn.error`. `run.test.ts` turn-error case pins the phase, seeded red then green.
- `fix(playbooks): a film of a run benched with --approve all no longer closes on "every card waited for a person's signature, and nothing was sent".` ADR 0055, ADR 0040 (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): the narration's closing line was fixed text, while `bench --approve all` signs every card and runs it on the page and bench.json records `approve`. It now says the run approved its cards and that nothing reached a real person. The 100 shipped benches are `none` and narrate as before. Tests: test_evidence (red before: the fixed line).
- `fix(accounts): a refused allow-list save gives the typed entries back instead of losing them with the box it emptied on Add.` README 4, ADR 0062 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): the draft was cleared before the daemon answered. `interact.test.tsx` pins refusal and success, seeded red then green.
- `fix(hirelane): the contrast check scores the gated panel's inks against the panel's own light sheet and fire button, which no run had ever measured.` DESIGN-LAW §7.1, §9 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker, parity-auditor; quality-gates): `check-contrast.mjs` modelled only the dark room, so the irreversible acts' text was never scored. Floors now name their ground; the sheet is read stop by stop. `--bd-gate-ink-2` measures 4.76:1 under 12px text and is floored at AA with AAA owed (backlog). `test/contrast-gate.test.ts`, three seeds red then green.
- `fix(stores): an origin forgotten in the other window, or whose first answer failed to save, is first sight again rather than switched off.` README 3.3, ADR 0026, 0063 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor; client-state/invalidation-strategy): `loadAll()` merged the table into the old `records` and `load()` cached an `enabled: false` blank, so `disabledOriginsOf`, the one reader that does not filter by `known`, kept refusing an app every other reader called first sight. `records` is now the table's rows only. New `origins.test.ts`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(apps): an address that names no origin stays in the register box with a reason, instead of being cleared while nothing is registered.` README 3.4 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): the module's register drops a URL with no origin silently, and the box had already emptied. `interact.test.tsx` pins it, seeded red then green.
- `fix(playbooks): the playbook loader classes a portal's tools by the manifest flags themselves, and its period table is pinned to the ranker's.` ADR 0040, ADR 0056 (a) (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `spec.KINDS` was a hand copy of `manifests.FLAGS` "the same table as" by comment, and `spec.PER_TABLE` and `rank.PERIODS` were two hand lists of one table with no test between them; the "every per is accepted" test typed five of the six and left out `week`. KINDS is FLAGS now; tests pin both (red before: KINDS is not FLAGS; a seventh period in one list went unnoticed).
- `fix(hirelane): the brief's type-voice check reads the faces the layout imports, so a retired face named in a layout comment no longer passes as loaded.` DESIGN-LAW §6 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist; quality-gates): `brief.test.ts` matched faces against `app/layout.tsx`'s raw text, which says in a comment that Caveat is gone. It now reads the `next/font/google` import, comments out, both ways. New case red then green; a seeded "Caveat" claim fails.
- `fix(stores): a settings row that cannot be read no longer leaves the store unhydrated, so neither window waits for ever on a blank first scene.` README 3.5 (scan-sweep stabilize, state-coverage, error-handler; client-state/rehydration-narrowing): `readRows()` read every row in one `Promise.all`, so one rejected read kept `hydrated` false and both windows blank, and the store:changed listener was never registered. Each row is read on its own, a failed one keeps its value and is named in `problem`. `settings.test.ts`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(stores): a tab's older tool-list answer that arrives last no longer overwrites the newer one about the same document.` README 3.4 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter; client-state/async-race-guards): `tools.refresh()` guarded only on the url, so two asks of one page (two toolchange announcements) could land out of order and leave the older, shorter list in place. A per-tab latest-wins token now decides. `tools.test.ts`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(playbooks): a forbidden tool must be one a card could name: registered, gated and not also a target, or the playbook does not load.` ADR 0040 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, bounty-hunter): truth's targets were checked for a registered, gated tool and `forbidden` was taken as is, so a misspelt, ungated or targeted name (or a bare string, read letter by letter) loaded and its rule could never fire. The authoring fixture that targeted its own forbidden tool is corrected. Tests: test_playbooks (four cases, red before: each loaded).
- `fix(hirelane): send_rejection refuses a wording the app does not have, instead of throwing and reporting a gated send's outcome as unknown.` DESIGN-LAW §7.7 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler, parity-auditor): the action passed any template to the letter, which threw, and the board reports a throw as "outcome unknown". It now refuses, naming the three wordings, as move_stage and decide_stage refuse a stage. `actions.test.ts` case, seeded red then green.
- `fix(stores): Check again while an engine check runs keeps reading Checking until the last answers, and the older answer no longer wins.` README 3.1, UAT B4 (scan-sweep stabilize, code-optimizer, state-coverage; client-state/async-race-guards): overlapping `useEngines.check()` calls cleared `checking` at the first answer and let the last to land write its probes, however old. A latest-wins token decides. New `engines.test.ts`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(hirelane): the app stylesheet's note on the ground colour points at the wrapper that exists, not at the removed app/v routes.` (scan-sweep stabilize, documentation-auditor, copy-auditor): `app/globals.css` placed the direction's wrapper "under app/v", a tree removed when the board became the root route; it is the `.h-root` `Board.tsx` mounts. Stale `app/v` references in app/components/lib 1 -> 0.
- `fix(playbooks): evidence --verify reads a narration entry that is not an object as a fault, not a crash that reports no playbook at all.` ADR 0055 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler, state-coverage): the media entries were read defensively but the narration comparison called `.get` on whatever the index held, so a string, a null or a list raised AttributeError out of `verify`. Tests: test_evidence (three shapes, red before: AttributeError).
- `fix(stores): a refused re-registration before an answer is written down, as another tab's refused manifest already is.` README 3.3 (scan-sweep stabilize, observability-auditor): `answerOne()` swallowed the focused page's refused manifest with `.catch(() => undefined)`, leaving no trace of what explains a later "a different app" refusal, while `publishOthers()` logs its own. Both log now. `run.test.ts`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(playbooks): a playbook whose app, phase, target or eligible item is not an object, or whose nudges is not a whole number, is reported with its other problems instead of raising out of the loader.` ADR 0040 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): the loader promises every problem as a sentence, but these five shapes raised AttributeError, ValueError or TypeError, so `check` printed a traceback and named no playbook. Tests: test_playbooks (five cases, red before: each raised).
- `fix(proving): the bench's and the Characters' copies of the desktop run loop's continuation line and bound are read against apps/desktop/src/stores/run.ts.` README §14, ADR 0040 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `scene.CONTINUE` ("verbatim from run.ts") and `bench.MAX_CONTINUATIONS` ("the desktop run loop's bound") were typed by hand with no test between them and the TypeScript. Tests: test_playbooks reads run.ts (red when either Python copy is changed).
- `fix(waking): the first act's address field opens a bare host as the apps register does, and says why when it cannot.` README 3.1, 3.4 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor; form/validation-timing): "mail.example.com" went raw to `tabs.create`, Rust's parser refused it and the refusal was only logged. The field now normalises like the register, refuses text with no origin with a reason and keeps it. New `waking/interact.test.tsx`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(surface): the Mind light stands the engine by the Setup module's rule, so it no longer says ready when the daemon failed or still runs on the old engine.` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0085 and 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, state-coverage): the light read ready off the probe alone while Mind reads `engineFact`; a failed check read "checking the engine" for ever. It now reads `engineFact`'s standing. New `surface/live.test.tsx`, 3 of 5 red before, 5 of 5 after.
- `fix(tidycrm): the wheel no longer slides the scene when the zoom is already at its bound.` (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter; canvas-graph/viewport-transform): `useWheelZoom` solved the pan for the unclamped zoom and `rig.set` then clamped only the zoom, so ten notches at the minimum flung the pan 13.5 world units toward the pointer. The step is a pure `wheelPose` that passes the rig's `BOUNDS.zoom` to the kit's `zoomAt`. New `test/blocks-ui.test.ts`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(ledgerbox): every tool the page registers is driven through its own handler, and a read that reaches an act or a write wearing another tool's class fails the suite.` README 3.3, design 5.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist; mcp-tools/tool-schema-design, negative-control-tests): no test ran a handler, so a read calling `voidInvoiceAction` and `categorize` under `registration("mark_paid")` both passed all 44 tests. `test/register.mjs` loads a JSX-free `.tsx` and takes scoped stand-ins; new `test/tool-handlers.test.ts` drives all 19 handlers through the kit's door against a scratch seed, with a built-in lying-table control. Both seeds red, then 46 of 46.
- `fix(mind): the Engine instrument's line no longer says the chosen engine runs the turns while the daemon is on another engine or failed on it.` README 3.5, ADR 0029 and 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, copy-auditor): `engineSentence` said "runs the turns" for any found probe while the tile's badge, from `engineFact`, said "not yet" or "needs you". Until the daemon runs on it the line is `engineFact`'s summary. `mind/view.test.tsx` case, red before, green after.
- `fix(waking): a microphone nobody has asked about reads "not asked yet", not "checking · not asked yet".` README 3.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor; async-ui-states/state-model): the first act's badge for an unknown fact said a check was running before anyone had pressed Check my microphone. `waking/view.test.tsx`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(tidycrm): the merge's consent names the phone and activity date the survivor takes from the folded record.` (scan-sweep stabilize, ux-reviewer; hitl-approval/consent-gates): the dossier's merge confirmation promised the survivor's value for every field the two disagree on, and `merge_contacts` said nothing, while the merge adopts a valid phone and a newer activity date from the folded record (22 of 60 open seed pairs). `test/blocks-ui.test.ts`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(mind): the field's Voice card says which rung of the voice failed, in the voice store's own reason, not that voice is not installed.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, registry-conformance voice-io/voice-ux-integration, copy-auditor): `voiceSentence` discarded `voiceReason` and called every unavailable voice "not installed". `mind/view.test.tsx` case, red before, green after.
- `fix(waking): Next into a step whose Wake her is not ready keeps the keyboard on the way forward instead of dropping it on the page.` README 3.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker; accessibility/a11y-verification): the not-ready Wake her is the reason's wrapper, a different element, so the pressed Next left the page and focus fell to the body. `waking/interact.test.tsx`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(tidycrm): the dossier's auto bar no longer says every change lands in revisions, in printed backticks.` (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): the AUTO band printed "every change lands in \`revisions\`" with literal backticks, while Flag stale and Keep both records write no revision row; it now says every change is logged and undo can put it back. `test/blocks-ui.test.ts`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(ledgerbox): a tool that refuses says so as { ok: false, error } on every tool that can refuse, as the kit's door and open_invoice already did.` README 3.3 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, error-handler; mcp-tools/tool-schema-design, two error channels - the registry map's recorded deviation at BooksTools.tsx:217): read_invoice and all seven acts answered a refusal as a bare sentence, search_invoices as `{ error }` with no ok; 2 of 11 refusing tools carried the flag. One `refused` helper; successes unchanged. New refusal test in `test/tool-handlers.test.ts`, 9 bare before, 0 after.
- `fix(tidycrm): the dossier's close button and checks band call the table a table, not a block in a zone.` (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): the close button's accessible name was "Close this block and return to the zone", two words `BK_LEVELS` retired; it and the checks band now say table and database. `test/blocks-ui.test.ts`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(mind): the field's "could not check which engines are installed" note carries the Check again it tells the person to press.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor, ux-reviewer): the note said "Press Check again." on a field with no such button. `mind/interact.test.tsx` case, red before, green after.
- `fix(voice): the studio's act is a plain block inside the stage's <main>, not a second main landmark.` README 3.1, 3.5 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker; accessibility/primitive-level-a11y): the studio drew `<main>` inside `Stage`'s own `<main className="stage__scenes">`. `voice/view.test.tsx`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(tidycrm): the L0 caption says "1 record" and "1 deviation outstanding", not "1 records" and "1 deviations".` (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): `databaseSummary` pluralised tables only. `test/blocks-ui.test.ts`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(surface): the Chores light says it could not count when the shipped playbooks failed to load, instead of "counting…" for ever.` README 3.5, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep stabilize, state-coverage; async-ui-states/failure-states): a failed fetch and one on its way both read "counting…". `surface/live.test.tsx` case, red before, green after.
- `fix(tidycrm): undo's tool description names the two refusals 70d7708 added, so an agent undoes newest first.` (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): the description listed only the kit's refusals (not reversible, already undone), not a field changed again since or a pair whose record left the list. `test/blocks-ui.test.ts` pins all four and the count of `UndoRefused` sites, seeded red then green.
- `fix(voice): the studio's Hold to talk, focused, holds on the Space it shows instead of ignoring it.` README 3.1, ADR 0028 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, parity-auditor; voice-io/voice-ux-integration): the document keys give a focused button its own Space, a mouse take leaves this one focused, and it handled only the pointer, unlike the module's HoldToTalk. `voice/interact.test.tsx`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(tidycrm): the export tool names its file through lib/csv.ts exportFilename, as the route does.` (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): the tool typed the filename twice beside the rule the route's Content-Disposition uses. `test/blocks-ui.test.ts`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(ledgerbox): a select in which no reference is an invoice refuses, and the ticks a person made stand.` README 3.3 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter; mcp-tools/tool-schema-design): a list of only stale or misread references cleared the person's tick and answered "Ticked 0 of 2 requested". It now answers `{ ok: false, error }` with the reference hint; the empty list still clears. New test in `test/tool-handlers.test.ts`, red (setPicked reached) then green.
- `fix(surface): "Open her window to answer" says so in the error colour when her window does not come forward, instead of only logging it.` README 3.1, ADR 0026 and 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler, observability-auditor): `summon` swallowed `athenaShow()`'s rejection into the console. It returns the promise and the view tells the failure in the ask row's note. `surface/interact.test.tsx` case, red before, green after.
- `fix(tidycrm): the foot's deviation breakdown walks DEVIATION_KINDS instead of a hand-typed copy.` (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): `KIND_ORDER` restated the model's four kinds, so a fifth would be counted everywhere but the breakdown. `test/blocks-ui.test.ts`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(ledgerbox): with a client chosen, the legend's live region says how many Everything lit, not that it shows all 125.` (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker, copy-auditor): `legendSay("all", ...)` ignored the lit count, so with one client chosen it announced "Showing all 125 invoices." over 9 lit and 116 dimmed. It now says "Every state, one client: 9 of 125 invoices lit, the rest dimmed." New test in `test/lanes.test.ts`, red then green.
- `fix(mind): Enter and "Use this" on the brain path follow one rule, so neither writes a path that is already stored and a written path stops reading as a draft.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, bounty-hunter): Enter wrote what the disabled button refused, and the untrimmed draft kept a stored path reading as unsaved. `mind/interact.test.tsx`, two cases red before, green after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): an act that throws under an agent's call still toasts the person watching the page and re-reads the sheet.` README 3.3 (scan-sweep stabilize, observability-auditor, error-handler): `settle` toasted and refreshed only after the action answered, so a throw reached the agent as `tool failed` and the person saw nothing. It now toasts an error, refreshes and rethrows. New test in `test/tool-handlers.test.ts`, red ("the person saw: nothing") then green.
- `fix(voice): Space on the hear step opens the microphone only when a listener can hear the take, and the hero names Space only when it acts.` README 3.1, ADR 0028 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, copy-auditor; voice-io/voice-ux-integration): with no listener ready the Hold button was disabled but Space recorded a take that `recordStop` then dropped, and the hero invited Space on steps where it did nothing. `keys.test.ts`, `view.test.tsx`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(tidycrm): a table's card is announced name first, the order it prints in.` (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): the L1 card prints the name before the ident but its accessible name led with the ident. `test/blocks-ui.test.ts`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(ledgerbox): the view model's heat words are the books' own table, re-exported, not a second hand-typed copy.` (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor; one-authority-per-vocabulary): `model/filters.ts` and `lib/lanes/heat.ts` each held the five heat words; every surface and the agent's read layer read the model's copy and the table beside `heatOf` had no reader. The model now re-exports it. New identity test in `test/lanes.test.ts`, red then green.
- `fix(voice): a studio step change is read out, because the live region around the step's words outlives the step.` README 3.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker; accessibility/live-region-architecture): the polite region sat inside the block keyed by the step, so each step mounted a new region already holding its text, which readers do not announce. `voice/interact.test.tsx`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(ledgerbox): every state a tool can light has exactly one legend entry, checked in both directions, and the legend's comment states its real order.` (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist; gate-scope-is-not-report-scope): the legend test checked LEGEND against STATE_FILTERS one way only, so deleting the `void` entry left every test green; the comment claimed STATE_FILTERS' order, which LEGEND does not follow. Seeded red, then green.
- `fix(tidycrm): the foot's first crumb prints BK_LEVELS' L0 word instead of typing "The plate" again.` (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): the breadcrumb is the reader's print of the level names now that the rail is gone, and it hand-typed the word `read_view` gives an agent from `BK_LEVELS`. `test/blocks-ui.test.ts`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(scenes): an engine card says "not checked", with its remedy, when the check could not run, instead of "checking" for ever.` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0085 and 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, state-coverage; async-ui-states/failure-states): `EngineSection` drew a failed probe and a failed start as a pending check. `shared/EngineSection.test.tsx`, red before, green after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): navigate's description says a client chosen with set_filter stays chosen, instead of promising the whole book.` (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor; mcp-tools/tool-schema-design): with one client chosen, `navigate("inbox")` lit 9 of 125 under a description that said "the whole book". The handler already kept the client and named it; the description now says so. Probe before and after.
- `fix(voice): the chord the Voice scene tells a person to hold is read against the one hotkeys.rs registers.` README 3.1, ADR 0026, 0027 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist): `HOTKEY` and the module's first words typed "Ctrl+Shift+Space" by hand beside `summon()` with no test between them. New `voice/parts.test.ts` reads the Rust; seeded red by changing the chord.
- `fix(ledgerbox): the tool and model comments state the counts and the amount path the code has, not 124 invoices and a string the door never lets through.` (scan-sweep stabilize, documentation-auditor): `model/sheet.ts` and `tools/LanesTools.tsx` said 124 invoices (the seed has 125), and the `mark_paid` handler's comment described refusing "1,250" though the schema's `number` type has the door refuse it first. Comments only; 3 false claims to 0.
- `fix(scenes): the Mind and surface stylesheet pins read a hover rule's declaration on comment-stripped CSS, not the selector's name anywhere in the file.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist, registry-conformance test-harness/pin-the-call-not-the-name): a comment naming `.mind__input:hover` or `.surface__summon:hover` kept both pins green with no hover. Seeded red, green after.
- `fix(voice): the wave's quiet line fades out as the bars rise instead of vanishing in one frame.` README 3.1, ADR 0089 (scan-sweep stabilize, ui-perfectionist; motion/continuity-across-change): `display: none` on the active wave swapped the idle line out while every sibling eases. Opacity on the fast token, none under Calm. `voice.css.test.ts`, seeded red then green; perf:voice pass.
- `fix(mind): the field's restart notice is the engine section's own, so it takes the density tokens and fades in like its twin instead of a one-off 10px/14px box that lands in one frame.` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, ui-perfectionist): `EngineSection` exports `RestartNoticeBar`, the field uses it. `mind/view.test.tsx` and `mind.css.test.ts`, red before, green after; `pnpm perf:mind` pass.
- `fix(voice): the studio's reopen-on-arrival rule is pinned: once per arrival, never before the done row is read.` README 3.5, ADR 0090, 0092 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist): nothing imported `scenes/voice/live.tsx`, so removing either guard left the suite green. New `voice/live.test.tsx`, six cases, each guard seeded red.
- `fix(mind): the Data level says in words that the file's place is not known yet, instead of naming the shell's store_path command to the person.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor): the path box printed "the shell has not answered store_path yet". `mind/view.test.tsx`, red before, green after; `docs/redesign/parity.md` rows updated.
- `fix(mind): the crumb's names for the Mind levels are pinned to the names the levels show, so the two hand-kept lists cannot drift.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 and 0090 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist, parity-auditor): `live.tsx` NAMES and `view.tsx` NAME had no test between them. `mind/view.test.tsx` case, seeded red, green after.
- `fix(voice): the settings test take ends when the window loses focus or settings is left, as the studio's take does.` README 3.1, ADR 0028 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, risk-assessor; voice-io/stt-pipeline): settings' keyboard-held test take had neither the studio's blur nor its unmount stop, so alt-tab or Escape mid-take left the microphone open. One hook, `useTakeEndsWithPlace`, for both. `voice/interact.test.tsx`, seeded red then green.
- `fix(surface): the Voice light says "not known yet" while the daemon is not up, as the Mind depth's Voice card does, instead of "not set up".` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, copy-auditor): the light called every unavailable voice "not set up", also before anyone had asked. `surface/live.test.tsx`, red before, green after.
- `fix(depth): a reload at a depth draws that depth's scene, not the surface the stage was mounted on.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, state-coverage): `startDepth` took the hash's place without counting it, and the stage, mounted at the surface before the root's effect ran, redraws its layers only on a new `seq`, so the bar said Mind / Engine over the surface's scene. `stage/Stage.dom.test.tsx` (new) and a `store.test.ts` case, both red before, green after.
- `fix(voice): a line the daemon began speaking as the key went down no longer takes the key, so the release still sends stop.` README 3.1, ADR 0019 and 0020 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, state-coverage; voice-io/duplex-agent-sessions): a voice.speaking that crossed the start frame set the phase to speaking, so release sent no stop and the person's words were lost. `stores/voice.test.ts`, red before, green after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): package.json declares the Node the tests and the books run on, ^22.18.0 || >=24, not >=22.5.` (scan-sweep stabilize, dependency-auditor, test-strategist; deployment-contract/platform-build-parity): the test hook needs `module.registerHooks` (22.15) and `.ts` loaded with no flag (22.18), the books need `node:sqlite` unflagged (22.13); `>=22.5` admitted all of 22.5-22.17. A test evaluates the declared range against that table at 15 versions; red before.
- `fix(voice): a take's column shows the newest take; an older take's transcript that lands late is dropped, as an older preview is.` README 3.1, ADR 0028 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, bounty-hunter; voice-io/stt-pipeline): a slow first take's answer could replace the second's. `modules/voice/live.test.ts`, red before, green after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): an act that throws under a person's click still tells them, re-reads the sheet and closes the gate it was armed through.` README 3.3 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, error-handler; hitl-approval, failure-not-empty-success): the card's `useRun` toasted only an answer, so a throwing action left no toast, no refresh and an armed gate, where the tool path's twin had caught it since cb124b4. New `test/ledgerbox-lanes-ui.test.ts`, red before, green after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): draft_reminder and send_reminder refuse an invoice nobody owes - a draft or a void - and say which.` (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, parity-auditor, copy-auditor): the seed's draft LB-2026-0055 got a dunning letter and send_reminder sent it; a void was refused as "settled"; a letter drafted before a void was still sent. One predicate over `COUNTED_OUT` now refuses all four with the real reason. Test red before, 4 of 4 refused after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): an armed gate closes when the letter, the amount or the state it showed changes under it, and when its act becomes refused.` README 5.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, ux-reviewer; hitl-approval/gate-state-machines, policy-tighten): Send it armed on one letter sent whatever draft was newest at the press, and Void it stayed armed after a credit landed. `Gate` is armed on a subject (`card/read.ts` gateSubject) and `stillArmed`; `test/ledgerbox-lanes-ui.test.ts`, red before, green after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): mark_paid and match_bank_line refuse an invoice nobody owes - a draft or a void - and say which.` (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, parity-auditor): $10.00, or a whole bank credit, could be recorded against the seed's draft, which no total counts; a void was refused by match only as "more than the $0.00 still owed". Both acts now read the same `COUNTED_OUT` predicate as the reminder acts. Test red before, 4 of 4 refused after.
- `fix(voice): one install read that fails no longer ends the poll and freezes the install bar for the visit.` README 3.1, ADR 0028 (scan-sweep stabilize, state-coverage, error-handler; voice-io/voice-setup-experience): the poll broke on the first failed GET /voice/install and the bar stayed busy, silent, while the daemon kept writing. `modules/voice/live.test.ts`, new `modules/voice/parts.test.tsx`, red before, green after.
- `fix(stage): the water's snow is repainted in the new accent when the theme changes, instead of keeping the one it was mounted under.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, visual-craft): `Backdrop` cached `--accent` once per mount and the settings resolve the theme after the stage mounts, so a light-theme launch drew the snow in the dark theme's cyan. A `data-theme` observer repaints once. `stage/Backdrop.dom.test.tsx` (new), red before, green after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): match_bank_line refuses a bank line of money paid out instead of booking it as a client's payment.` (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, security-auditor): the pool it drew from holds the expense debits too, stored positive, so the studio's coworking desk rent "Applied $240.00 to LB-2026-0002". A line whose direction is not `in` is now refused by name. Test red before, refused after with no payment written.
- `fix(stage): the surface names no empty "Where you are" landmark; the breadcrumb navigation exists only when it holds a crumb.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): the surface's bar carried an empty named `<nav>`; it is a plain box (same space, same drag region) until the first crumb. `Stage.test.tsx` case, red before, green after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): send_reminder re-checks the books and does not send a letter for an invoice paid, disputed or part paid since the draft.` (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, parity-auditor; hitl-approval/resume-after-decision): a draft, then a payment in full, then the send told the client "$25,350.00 outstanding, 70 days past due". The chase rule is now one function both acts ask, and the send refuses a letter whose recomposed figures differ from the stored draft. Test red before, 3 of 3 stale letters refused after.
- `fix(voice): a voice-setup answer whose body cannot be read is a refusal, never a throw, as the client's contract says.` README 3.1, ADR 0028 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler, parity-auditor; async-ui-states): preview, transcribe and the JSON routes read the body outside the guard, so a dropped body or a 200 that is not JSON left a preview at "Getting ready..." for good. `lib/voice-setup.test.ts`, red before, green after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): the server actions refuse a category, tone or period outside their vocabulary, not only the tool layer.` (scan-sweep stabilize, security-auditor, parity-auditor, error-handler): only BooksTools checked the enums, so a direct call filed an invoice under "banana", threw out of draft_reminder on an unknown tone, and wrote an export of a period the books do not have. Each act now checks the constant the manifest publishes. Test red before, 4 of 4 refused after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): the decision list holds back, and says why, every act the books would refuse on that invoice, instead of offering a press that can only come back as an error.` README 5.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, ux-reviewer, copy-auditor; hitl-approval): Decide offered Draft it on both disputed invoices and Draft it and Record it on the unsent draft, 4 presses the server refuses. `card/read.ts` actRefusal; `test/ledgerbox-lanes-ui.test.ts` presses every act on every open invoice against the real actions, seeded red, 0 of 296 disagree after.
- `fix(voice): her reply's player resumes a suspended audio context, as the studio's preview player does.` README 3.1, ADR 0019, 0020 and 0027 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor; voice-io/voice-ux-integration, half of the recorded deviation): the live Player scheduled her reply into a suspended context the preview player would have resumed. `lib/voice.test.ts`, red before, green after.
- `fix(stage): a change in her presence or the bar no longer redraws every scene layer; a scene is drawn again only for its place or a new way of drawing it.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep stabilize, code-optimizer): every athena status, focused-tab or crumb-name change re-rendered the stage and with it each layer's whole scene; a memo'd `SceneOf` per layer. `Stage.dom.test.tsx`, 3 draws before, 1 after; `perf:stage` passes.
- `fix(depth): a hash that changes to one that is not a place is rewritten to where the window is, as it already is at start.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): the start canonicalised the hash and the hashchange handler did not, so a junk hash while the window stood where it resolved stayed in the URL. `store.test.ts` case, red before, green after.
- `fix(voice): a running install is a progress bar with its value, and its live region says the phase, not a figure that moves four times a second.` README 3.1, ADR 0028 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker; accessibility, async-ui-states): the polite live region held the percentage the 250 ms poll moves, and the bar had no role. `modules/voice/parts.test.tsx`, red before, green after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): match_bank_line says a line id is not in the books, or which invoice the line is already on, instead of "applied elsewhere".` (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor, error-handler): a mistyped line id and a line already on the same invoice both got "already applied elsewhere", a cause that was not theirs. The miss now names it: no such line, or the invoice numbers the line is on. Test red before, 3 of 3 after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): mark_paid refuses a fraction of a cent instead of rounding it into a permanent payment.` (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, bounty-hunter): `parseDollars` refuses a sub-cent amount, but the action rounded what a direct caller sent, so 1000.5 cents was recorded as $10.01. It now refuses a non-integer cent amount. Test red before, refused after.
- `fix(voice): Enter in the OpenAI key field sends nothing while a key is being saved, as Save key does not.` README 3.1, ADR 0028 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor, ux-reviewer; form): the button refused while busy and Enter sent a second PUT /voice/key. New `modules/voice/pieces.test.tsx`, red before, green after.
- `test(ledgerbox): the lanes-ui tests write only to their scratch books, and refuse to run anywhere else.` (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist, risk-assessor; test-harness): top-level awaits between `test()` calls let tempHome's `after` hook fire mid-file, so the later tests ran on the dev server's own `data/ledgerbox.sqlite`. Imports hoisted, a `books()` guard seeded red; the 95 rows written there were reversed and the file diffed clean against a fresh seed.
- `fix(ledgerbox): the page's athena:app identity tags read APP_ID and APP_VERSION instead of restating them as literals.` (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): the bridge reads the page's identity from these tags while the books are filed under APP_ID, and `app/layout.tsx` typed both out again, so a rename or a version bump in `lib/constants.ts` would leave the page announcing the old one. Two copies to one; a source test pins it, red before.
- `fix(voice): inside a button the key chip and the hold dot sit the button's 6px gap from the label, not 12 and 14.` README 3.1, ADR 0028 (scan-sweep stabilize, ui-perfectionist; design-tokens): voice.css margins were added on top of `.btn`'s flex gap. `modules/voice/voice.css.test.ts`, red before, green after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): a lane that loses the invoice its tab stop was on keeps one tab stop, at L0 and at L1, instead of none.` (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker, state-coverage): both roving rows compared the index to a remembered stop, so refiling a lane's latest-due invoice left all 6 lanes with no Tab stop. `swarm/marks.ts` tabStop; `test/ledgerbox-lanes-ui.test.ts`, red before, green after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): an armed gate's confirm is described by its own question, so a screen reader hears what the yes writes or sends.` README 5.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker; hitl-approval): the confirm took focus with no tie to the sentence beside it, so 0 of 3 gated confirms were announced with their disclosure. `aria-describedby` to the question; `test/ledgerbox-lanes-ui.test.ts`, red before, green after.
- `fix(ledgerbox): undoing an unmatch refuses to re-apply a bank line now on another invoice, or past what is still owed.` (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, parity-auditor; hitl-approval/resume-after-decision): the undo put the match and payment back without asking where the line was now, so one credit could pay two invoices, or push a balance below zero. It now refuses with the reason and writes nothing. Two tests, red before.
- `fix(stage): Escape is left to a scene that claims it (a topic's lightbox, a studio's draft question) instead of the stage rising first.` README 3.1 and 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, parity-auditor): the stage and the scenes all listened on the document and the stage, registered first, always heard the key first, so the Adopt studio and the intake left an unsent draft without asking. The stage listens on the window. `Stage.dom.test.tsx`, red before, green after; `perf:stage` rises with a real Escape.
- `fix(voice): the studio's last line is pinned to the hotkey the Voice scene shows, so the two spellings cannot drift.` README 3.1, ADR 0028 and 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist, parity-auditor): `model.ts` wrote "Ctrl+Shift+Space" by hand beside the scene's HOTKEY, with no test between them. `modules/voice/model.test.ts`, seeded red, green after.
- `docs(ledgerbox): the lanes UI's comments state the labelling rule, the counts and the refs the code has, not a width threshold, 124 invoices and a shared focus-trap node.` (scan-sweep stabilize, documentation-auditor, copy-auditor): 7 present-tense claims the code contradicts, 0 after; comments only.
- `fix(stage): a crumb whose place changes on an across move fades in with the move instead of swapping its words in one frame.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep stabilize, ui-perfectionist): crumbs were keyed by depth, so Apps to Chores rewrote the crumb in place; keyed by the place they name, a new place plays the existing `bar-in`. `Stage.dom.test.tsx`, red before, green after; `perf:stage` passes.
- `fix(ledgerbox): the decision list draws a Gate exactly where the class table says GATED, read from the same isAuto as the badge, instead of a control typed by hand per row.` README 5.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor; hitl-approval): the badge read lib/tool-classes.ts while the five controls beside it were hand-typed, so a reclassified act would wear Gated over a one-press button. One render branch; `test/ledgerbox-lanes-ui.test.ts`, red before, seeded control.
- `fix(voice): a studio row that cannot be read is logged with its reason, not swallowed, before the studio opens again.` README 3.1, ADR 0028 (scan-sweep stabilize, observability-auditor; error-handling): `readDone().catch(() => null)` reopened the studio for a person who had finished it with no trace. `modules/voice/live.test.ts`, red before, green after.
- `fix(stage): the safety flush that settles a move a hidden window never plays, and the settle it reports, are pinned by a test.` README 3.1, ADR 0087 and 0089 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist): the stage's `SAFETY_MS` flush and its `onSettle` had no test. `Stage.dom.test.tsx` case, seeded red by a timer that dispatches nothing.
- `fix(ledgerbox): the decision list says whether Athena is connected from the page's own reading, as the footer does, instead of "not connected" in ink.` (scan-sweep stabilize, copy-auditor, parity-auditor): with a bridge in the page the footer said connected and the dialog said not. `card/read.ts` talkSay over `useAthenaPresence`; `test/ledgerbox-lanes-ui.test.ts`, red before, green after.
- `fix(depth): the model drops ROOT_MODULE, a root-to-module map nothing reads.` README 3.5, ADR 0087 (scan-sweep stabilize, tech-debt-tracker): `git grep ROOT_MODULE` found only its definition; 0 after, typecheck green.
- `fix(demo-kit): under test, openDb refuses any file outside the scratch home, so a reaper that fires mid-file cannot aim later tests at the dev books.` ADR 0017 (scan-sweep stabilize, risk-assessor; test-harness): tempHome's root `after` restored the cwd mid-file across a top-level await and later tests opened the app's own `data/` database. `DB_SCRATCH_ENV`, set by all three apps' `test/tmp-home.ts`; `examples/ledgerbox/test/scratch-guard.test.ts` (seeded 50 ms await, red before) and `examples/demo-kit/test/db-scratch.test.ts`.
- `fix(halo): a caption the linger timer has taken stays gone, instead of coming back over the screen when the next turn starts thinking or listening.` README 3.1, ADR 0027 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, state-coverage): `stepCaption` re-held an expired line in any holding phase, so a typed turn or a fresh press showed her reply from minutes ago. `halo/model.ts` drops an expired line before folding; `main.ts` leaves a taken line under its fade. `halo/model.test.ts` case, red before, green after.
- `fix(hirelane): the gated panel's 12px class sentence meets the AAA its size is owed, 7.49:1 instead of 4.76:1.` DESIGN-LAW §7.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker; design-tokens): `--bd-gate-ink-2` #8a5c52 to #63423b, and `design/check-contrast.mjs` floors it at 7.0 like every 12px ink. `test/contrast-gate.test.ts` seeds the old value, red before, green after.
- `fix(halo): a frame the shell refused is sent again on the next change, instead of being remembered as sent.` README 3.1, ADR 0027 (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler): the producer's dedupe key was set before `athena_halo` answered, so a refused idle frame kept the overlays on the phase before it. `lib/halo.ts` forgets a refused key; `lib/halo.test.ts` case, red before, green after.
- `fix(hirelane): the foot's register disclosure takes the room's focus ring, like the Back pill beside it.` DESIGN-LAW §1 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker, ui-perfectionist): `summary` joins the room's `:focus-visible` list in `base/the-room.css`. `test/sheets.test.ts` derives the focusable tags from the board's components; red before (`summary` unmatched), green after.
- `fix(tidycrm): every tool the Blocks register is driven through its own handler, and a read that changes the sheet or moves the view unannounced fails the suite.` README 3.3, design 5.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist; mcp-tools/tool-schema-design, test-harness/negative-control-tests): no test ran a handler in `components/blocks/tools/`; `test/spatial-model-tools.test.ts` loads `BlocksTools.tsx` as shipped, runs the kit's real zoom verbs beside it, and drives all seven through `runTool` on a scratch seed, with a built-in lying-tool control.
- `fix(tidycrm): search_blocks' coverage_under filters on the coverage figure its own results print, so a table printed at 72 comes back for coverage_under 72.` README 3.3 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor; mcp-tools/tool-schema-design): the filter compared the raw fraction while every read printed it rounded, dropping 10 of 46 seeded tables from a filter at their own printed figure; `percent()` in `tools/read.ts` is now the one rounding both use.
- `fix(hirelane): the focus ring on the irreversible acts is drawn in the panel's ink, 15.34:1 instead of 1.46:1.` DESIGN-LAW §7.1 (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker): the gated zone's `:focus-visible` takes `--bd-gate-ink` in `level2/l2-dossier-3.css`; the room's accent measured 1.46:1 on the light sheet. `test/sheets.test.ts` scores every ring on every ground; red before, green after.
- `fix(halo): the caption's fade ends before the shell hides an idle halo, and a test ties the page's timings to HIDE_AFTER.` README 3.1, ADR 0027 (scan-sweep stabilize, parity-auditor): linger 4000 ms plus a 600 ms caption fade overran `HIDE_AFTER` (4500 ms). The caption fades over the edges' 400 ms; `halo/halo.css.test.ts` reads `halo.rs`, `model.ts` and `halo.css`, red before (4600 > 4500), green after.
- `fix(hirelane): an open capability register no longer paints the foot and its list over an open dossier.` DESIGN-LAW §7.3 (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter, state-coverage; modal-stack): the foot's z-index raise in `base/the-room.css` now excludes a mounted `.bd-dossier-layer`, which lives inside the z-2 stage. `test/sheets.test.ts` derives the stage's level; red before, green after.
- `fix(tidycrm): the four named camera poses are recognised and snapped to however many turns the reader has orbited left, not only right.` (scan-sweep stabilize, bounty-hunter): `snapNear` and `poseName` wrapped yaw with a sign-keeping `%`, so after one full turn left 12 of 24 pose-and-turn cases went unnamed and unsnapped; both now use the kit's `angleDelta`, and `test/space.test.ts` walks every pose at -3 to 3 turns.
- `fix(halo): the three hues the halo spells by hand are pinned to the house tokens they copy.` README 3.1, ADR 0027 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist): `--halo-speak`, `--halo-think` and `--halo-gate` copy app.css's dark `--accent`, `--role-agent` and `--role-human` with nothing comparing them. `halo/halo.css.test.ts` case; 0 of 3 pinned before, 3 of 3 after, seeded red with a one-digit drift.
- `fix(hirelane): the dossier's close button answers a hover like the carousel arrows it was drawn from.` DESIGN-LAW §1.3 (scan-sweep stabilize, ui-perfectionist, visual-craft; ui-controls): `.bd-close` gains the arrow's hover and transition in `level2/l2-dossier.css`. `test/sheets.test.ts` reads the round-pill family from the sheets; red before (`.bd-close`), green after.
- `fix(tidycrm): a dossier write whose server action throws is toasted and disarms its gate, instead of escaping the transition to the error boundary.` (scan-sweep stabilize, error-handler, observability-auditor): `useRun` awaited the action with no catch, so a throw reached React's error boundary with no toast and left an armed merge armed; it is now logged and answered as a refusal through the same toast and `after`, pinned in `test/spatial-model-tools.test.ts`.
- `fix(hirelane): the carousel's current dot eases as the loupe moves instead of scaling 1.4x in one frame.` DESIGN-LAW §1.3 (scan-sweep stabilize, ui-perfectionist; motion): `.bd-dot` transitions transform and background on `--bd-dur-2` in `level1/l1-carousel-2.css`. `test/sheets.test.ts` finds every state rule that sets a transform; red before (`.bd-dot[aria-current]`), green after.
- `fix(halo): the page's phase list and event name are pinned to the shell's parser, not to a second hand-kept list.` README 3.1, ADR 0027 (scan-sweep stabilize, test-strategist, parity-auditor): `HALO_PHASES` and `HALO_EVENT` mirror `src-tauri/src/halo.rs` with only a hand-enumerated Rust test between them. `lib/halo-signal.test.ts` reads the parser's arms; seeded red with an extra phase.
- `fix(tidycrm): Up and Down on a database's tables step a row or stay put, instead of sliding the focus sideways at the grid's edge.` (scan-sweep stabilize, accessibility-checker; accessibility): `gridStep` clamped a row step into range, so 22 Up/Down presses over one to seven tables changed column and Down then Up did not come back; an edge now keeps the focus where it is, pinned in `test/spatial-model-tools.test.ts`.
## Playbooks — the edge between difficulty and usefulness (in progress)

- `docs: module documentation in docs/features and a build report across the waves` — one page per module (core, harness, daemon and channels, connectors, bridge, desktop, playbooks, Proving Ground, examples and UAT) with its current state; `docs/report/index.html`, a standalone page of the five waves, the nine playbooks' latest runs, the twelve fixes and the limits.

- `feat(playbooks): a parent's estate across twelve portals, benched to exceeds` — the disclaimer before its nine months, the IRS first, claims allowed at the balance at death, a paid hospital claim refused, eleven traps; five runs that found ADRs 0050 to 0052.

- `feat(playbooks): a freelancer's receivables across three client portals, the bank and New York's freelancer law, benched to exceeds` — a Coupa PO flip, a demand under the Freelance Isn't Free Act where a contract overrides Ariba's Net 120, a W-9 that holds a Tipalti payment, and four things not to chase; four runs, ADRs 0048 and 0049 found on the way; run 4: $21,100 of $21,550, 0 false.

- `feat(companion): a handed-over playbook shows her plan before the first turn` — Talk's empty state becomes the plan: her steps, the tabs she will ask for, where she stops for a signature, from the bundled playbook by id; the "Working on" chip stays.

- `feat(playbooks): the overview's record says what she walked past, her time and the cost` — totals add the latest runs' traps, false claims, minutes, the hours by hand and the model's cost; six stats, each with its context line.

- `feat(playbooks): the grid filters by whose chore it is and orders by edge, money or hours` — `audience` in playbook.json (home or work); `arrange()` keeps each tile's number from the map; two `PillGroup` radio groups, counted.

- `feat(playbooks): a parent's long-term-care claims across six portals, benched to exceeds` — a daily maximum that steps on the anniversary and spans the agency and the adult day center, inpatient and Medicare-paid days, a granddaughter's Zelles, a rejected invoice and an open appeal beside a closed one, a capped home modification and waived premiums; $26,410 found on both runs, 0 false.

- `feat(playbooks): the traps she walked past, by name` — `score()` keeps a `trap_ledger` (every trap, why, filed or not); the layer's "What she was right to leave alone", a filed trap first and in the error colour, folded past five; every shipped bench.json backfilled with its counts unchanged.

- `feat(playbooks): watch the run — bench.json keeps each turn, and the playbook's layer replays it` — `trace_of` (portal, the person's words or the run loop's continuation, her words cut at a sentence with `said_chars`, reads, cards with their outcomes); the layer's "Watch the run": a rail of tab visits with a dot per turn coloured by what it did, play, step, arrow keys, the money found so far; every shipped bench.json backfilled from its saved report.

- `feat(playbooks): the trucking accessorials desk, six portals and a window that closes today, benched to exceeds` — detention, lumper, layover and TONU checked against rate cons, BOLs and the ELD and filed in each broker's own tool; run 2: 9 of 9, $1,580, 13 of 13 traps avoided, after ADR 0047.
- `feat(playbooks): the subcontractor's lien desk, benched to exceeds` — lien and notice deadlines in CA and TX, waivers with their exceptions and a clean pay app across the office, Procore, Textura and GCPay; six runs, the last 10 of 10 for $416,700 after two corrections to the bench's own world.

- `feat(playbooks): distributor deductions for a food brand, across UNFI, KeHE, the Drive and the warehouse, benched to exceeds` — six deductions over-taken or duplicated and one with no proof it ran ($10,882), beside eight valid ones, two expired and one only KeHE's advanced search shows; run 2: 7 of 7, every amount exact, 0 false, 4.2 minutes.

- `feat(playbooks): medical bills against the EOBs and denied clinic claims, benched to exceeds, and what each taught Athena` — two playbooks with their run histories (medical: seven runs, $388 to $3,423.50; clinic: $0 to $2,120); `lessons` and `caveat` in playbook.json, shown in the layer and counted on the overview; README §14 records every fix the runs found.
- Fixes found by the runs: ADR 0041 (refusals and drops told in the turn, with calls in flight), ADR 0042 (recall: any word, the matched region), ADR 0043 (tool results by budget), ADR 0044 (the playbook rides every turn), ADR 0045 (evidence is what a page says; gather in each tab), the OP grammar's one shape; ADR 0029's three surfaces built (Connectors, Setup, Browser).

- `feat(playbooks): a use case is data, proven on the gate by a real Athena, and the desktop shows the edge` — `playbooks/<id>/{playbook,world,truth,bench}.json`; `athena.proving.playbooks` (spec, simulated portals, bench, score from cards, prose audit, `check`/`bench`/`rescore`); the first playbook, Amazon FBA reimbursements, benched live (exceeds: 5 of 5, 14 of 14 traps avoided, $1.47); the Playbooks module with the edge map, tiles and a layer; `Tile` and `Layer` (ADR 0029's first callers); "Hand it to Athena" puts the command in her composer through `athena:offer`; ADR 0040.

## End to end — the seams, driven for the first time (in progress)

- `fix(desktop): the panel never published a manifest, and three other seams the fakes hid` — `lib/manifest.ts` publishes the focused page's tools through `manifestOf`, without which `POST /run` refused every real turn `foreign_origin` before the stream opened; `GET /decisions` answers `pending`, not `decisions`; `TabTools` carries the app version a manifest needs; `apps/desktop/e2e/` drives the real client, run loop, connector store and voice socket against a real daemon on an ephemeral port.
- `ci: the gate runs the suites it can run` — the javascript job stops iterating `@athena/journey`, a browser end-to-end suite that boots four servers and wants a Chromium the runner does not install.

## The hands (in progress)

- `feat(desktop): the ninth hand takes the picture` — `page_screenshot` as a hand the shell answers rather than the page, dispatched off a `runner` field so `hands.js` stays the whole of what a page can do; `PrintWindow` with `PW_RENDERFULLCONTENT` cropped to `layout`'s page rectangle, through two coordinate conversions — the scale factor and the window frame's inset; a non-focused tab refused with `validator_failed` naming the tab that is focused; `Store::put_capture` files the PNG through the same writer as everything else and the hand answers with the row id; `tabs::origin_of` and `Tabs::url_for`; `ATHENA_SMOKE_HANDS=1` runs one hand of each kind against a real window; ADR 0025.

## Connectors (in progress)

- `feat(connectors): Gmail and Notion behind one vault, entering the catalog like a page` — one JSON spec per service; the vault brokers every call with a host allow-list, a timeout, a cap and redaction; a token or grant is probed before it is sealed (keyring, DPAPI or an owner-only file); PKCE over a one-shot loopback listener; writes behind a switch and an egress allow-list, reads fenced and capped; `/connectors` routes; `Policy(connectors=vault)`; ADR 0021.
- `feat(desktop): the Connectors module` — one card per service: a standing derived at render, the seal sentence, reads and writes with GATED on writes only, the writes switch, the allow-list saved on purpose, a token field or the OAuth client pair with the consent flow's phase, the guide folded; `stores/connectors.ts` mirrors the daemon and keeps no credential; `connectors.css`.

## Desktop — the second pass (in progress)

- `feat(desktop): the Browser is about apps, and its ledger is the origins table` — the doorway when nothing is open, a ledger of registered apps with a standing derived from the tabs and the relay, register-then-open; `browser.css`; ADR 0024.
- `feat(desktop): the Panel is a conversation` — a 46rem column, bubbles and prose, deltas grouped in the model, activity folded to chips, cards at the end of the stream, the tool table as a disclosure, a docked composer, suggestions from the page's read tools, the voice partial in the composer; `panel.css`.
- `feat(desktop): Setup and Settings become one module` — one view-model with an onboarding letter and a settings list, the restart wired to the daemon store, readiness without stations; the settings directory removed; `setup.css`.
- `feat(desktop): the ledger is the Browser's page` — the doorway illustration removed; the register field first, at title size while the ledger is empty; rows as a grid with a monogram tile lit only for an app that answered with tools, the standing as dot and word and sentence, the tools cell, last seen, and acts quiet until hover or focus; the focused tab's row carries a left rule.
- `feat(desktop): the Setup module draws its facts` — seven inline SVG glyphs and a hero illustration in token strokes; a passage's glyph takes the primary hue only when the machine has that fact in hand; the settings rows carry a quiet 22px glyph beside the dot.
- `fix(desktop): the run loop holds a host call until the turn ends` — a gated proposal arrives as a `tool.call` followed by a `pending_approval` result and the card; running it on arrival ran the gated action before the answer. Calls are held, a result releases them, what remains at `turn.finished` runs.
- `chore(desktop): the mark in the bar, the icon set, and the stylesheet without the old panel and letter` — the reference build's logo as the window, taskbar and bar mark; the dead `.transcript`/`.composer`/`.panel-card`/`.passage`/`.rail` blocks removed.

## P8 — Voice (in progress)

- `feat(voice): the gateway and one backend` — `athena.channels.voice`: RFC 6455 in the stdlib, `/voice` on the daemon's port with the token in the header or the `athena-token.<token>` subprotocol, the `VoiceBackend` port with a scripted backend and an OpenAI one over `urllib`; an utterance runs the ordinary browser-lane turn with `surface = voice`, `trigger = voice`; the `TTS:` line or the text capped and announced; barge-in by generation counter; "approve" / "decline" / a label answers the card on top through `decide`; the `voice` event family and `TurnContext.trigger`; ADR 0019.
- `feat(desktop): push-to-talk` — a hold-to-talk key in the module bar and `Ctrl+Space` held in the chrome; the microphone as PCM16 at 16 kHz over `/voice` with the token in the subprotocol; a spoken turn lands in the panel's transcript and cards; the page's tools run through the relay and answer the daemon; the player drops a generation on `barge_in`; the Setup wizard gains a microphone passage, never required; the OS-global shortcut deferred and named; ADR 0020.

## Examples — the studio and the journey (in progress)

- `feat(examples): the shared kit and the studio's world` — `examples/` joins the workspace; `@athena/demo-kit` pruned to `db`, `activity`, `seed`, `ui`, `zoom`, `webmcp`; the seed registry gains `STUDIO`, one billing contact per client and the named cross-app people and aliases; ADR 0017 narrows the example-app non-goal to the three the demo runs on.
- `feat(examples): ledgerbox, the books of act 1` — the Strip at `/`, variants removed; reads carry the client contact, `paid_ratio`, ambiguity and short-by on credits, the bank alias on a match; drafts are whole messages from the studio inbox; the close exports a titled markdown page.
- `feat(examples): hirelane, the pipeline of act 2` — the Board at `/` with both tool sets mounted; `read_applicants` and `read_shortlist`; the Kestrel Labs applicant pinned from the registry; scheduling and rejection mail as complete envelopes from the studio inbox; no protected column, no protected parameter.
- `feat(examples): tidycrm, the contact list of act 3` — the Blocks at `/`, the AG-UI proxy removed; `read_conflicts`, `preview_company`, `resolve_company` through the revisions log; the registry contacts as anchors; a `clients` segment and a titled markdown export.
- `feat(examples): the journey, four acts run end to end` — `@athena/journey` boots the three apps in scratch directories, drives them through `inject.js` over the `athena-webmcp` protocol, classifies with `gate.js`, approves and declines through a surface fake, models mail and notes connectors behind egress allow-lists, and prints the ledger and the facts it cites.
- `feat(examples): ledgerbox ships The Lanes` — the newer direction restored from the reference repository as the root page; one manifest carrying the view layer and the books layer, 23 tools, three gated; the page reads the bridge's presence marker; ADR 0018.
- `fix(examples): the journey records itself on request, and the example-apps ADR is 0017` — `JOURNEY_VIDEO=1` records the run from the spec's own context (the config option a history rewrite carried in never applied); the ADR renumbered out of the 0014 collision.
- `chore(examples): one React types version, and the review cameras` — the four example packages move to the React types the desktop package pins, so react-three-fiber augments the copy Tidycrm compiles against; per-app screenshot scripts under `examples/journey/scripts/`.
- `feat(examples): ledgerbox readability pass` — a status component for presence, 12px floor on labels and legend, labelled view toggles moved beside the level rail, AUTO and GATED action bands, a scrolling aside at 1024.
- `feat(examples): hirelane readability pass` — the carousel overflow fixed, the handwriting face removed, one 12px sentence-case label style, avatar clusters with an overflow count, a live headline and presence line, a capability pill with a disclosure instead of raw tool names, AUTO and GATED as one class component; brief and law amended.
- `feat(examples): tidycrm readability pass` — one 13px sentence-case label style and one stat scale at all three depths, zone and block cards led by the name, deviations named as deviations, chips for the breakdown and the zone jump, a presence line, AUTO and GATED as one band component.
- `feat(examples): hirelane contrast is measured, and the label tier carries the accent` — `design/check-contrast.mjs` composites the translucent planes and scores every token against the worst of them, chained into `lint`; the ink ladder retuned to clear AAA on the three text rungs and AA on the fourth; `--bd-accent` spent where the brief always declared it, on the level rail's rung and the region titles, and kept out of the AUTO/GATED legend and the light gated panel; `design/ui-pass-brief.md` specifies the L1 and L2 layers still to come; ADR 0023.
- `feat(examples): hirelane's dossier is one action bar` — the two stacked action panels become one bar of two zones, measured down from 230px to 95px and the head from 190 to 116, so the reading column goes from 29% of itself visible to 44%; the class sits above its controls so a long name cannot wrap the bar, arming hides the reversible zone and takes the whole row, the note becomes a disclosure instead of a permanent textarea, and the phone gets a full-height sheet with side-scrolling zones instead of a pane that was 87% chrome; ADR 0022.
- `feat(examples): the take — narrate, record, compose` — `narrate.mjs` (ElevenLabs, one clip per beat, measured durations), `tests/take.spec.ts` (the beats of `script/journey.en.json` played one at a time through the real bridge and gate, a surface strip the runner injects, act 4 rendered from the live ledger and brain, `take/take.json` offsets), `compose.mjs` (clips laid at offsets, captions, mp4); docs/demo.md section 1.
- `feat(examples): the two-minute cut` — `script/cut.en.json`, 18 beats keyed O1 to R2; the recorder plays the cut through the apps, the portal with the hands, and the record page with three tiers; `narrate.mjs` takes `JOURNEY_SCRIPT`; captions at a readable size; the delivered take is 2:01 with narration in three voices.
- `feat(examples): the take opens with onboarding` — O1 the letter rendered from a live `athena doctor` run (engines, brain, constitution; nothing fabricated, secrets redacted), O2 and O3 each page's manifest arriving as a card with the gated tools named, O4 the quiet books; counts and rows asserted against the surface and the doctor; a stale clip is dropped when its line changed.
- `feat(examples): cut B, concept first` — `script/cutb.en.json`: onboarding, one spoken command, one full gate exchange, the narrator carrying the three claims while Athena works; the take's decline count follows the script.

## P5 — MVP checkpoint (done)

- `fix(core): the capability block teaches the OP grammar it always assumed` — found by a live turn against a real CLI: the model read the block, hunted for a native tool API, and asked the user to run the tool instead.
- `feat(desktop): the panel module, an AG-UI client of the daemon` — `lib/api.ts` with `fetch` as an argument, `stores/run.ts` as the only run loop with its host-tool and approval continuations bounded at eight, the Panel module with the card's own parameters and the tool list; 14 headless tests against a fake daemon and a fake page.
- `fix(core): the approvals inbox is ordered by insertion, not by a random id` — five cards in one millisecond came back shuffled.
- `docs: the phase-1 status, written at the MVP checkpoint` — what runs, what only a hand-run found, how to launch it, and what P6 inherits.

## P4 — The shell skeleton

- `feat(desktop): the window, the module bar, and the browser module` — Tauri v2 multi-webview, `layout.rs` owns every rectangle, the module contract and `preview.html?module=&fixture=&theme=`, the house-style token layer; popups allowed after measuring that Tauri denies them by default; ADR 0013.
- `feat(desktop): the relay` — `inject.js` as every page webview's initialization script straight from the bridge package, a forwarder with the tab id and a nonce, `bridge_list` / `bridge_call` with the id map and the 35 s timer, `bridge_reply` as the page's only command, `bridge:toolchange`; the `ATHENA_SMOKE=1` line proven on a scratch page; ADR 0014.
- `feat(desktop): the sidecar, with exit hygiene` — a minted token file, the frozen binary or the `uv run athena serve` fallback, the bounded ready-line wait, the `/health` poll, restart on engine change, tree kill and a Windows job object with `KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE`; all three exit paths walked by hand; `scripts/build-sidecar.py`; ADR 0015.
- `feat(desktop): the store, settings and setup modules` — one SQLite store with every table now (settings, origins, projects, project_pages, project_runs, activity, captures with an LRU sweep), four generic commands with `null` as the only empty value, the Settings and Setup modules with four fixtures each; ADR 0016.

## P3 — One turn end to end, no browser

- `feat(harness): structural policy` — per-lane allow-list, origin enabled, origin pinning, `connector_enabled` asked on every call; `to_cedar()`; `PolicyHook` in front of the gate.
- `feat(lane): the browser lane` — one turn composed, run and streamed; gated calls become `decision.requested`; `answer_decision` replays the gate and returns `execute` from the row's own parameters; ADR 0010.
- `feat(daemon): the server, the token, and one request per connection` — `ThreadingHTTPServer`, the token on every route including `/health`, `Connection: close`, CORS with preflight, the ready line; the starvation test first; ADR 0011.
- `feat(daemon): manifest, run, decisions` — `POST /manifest`, `POST /run` as SSE, `POST /decisions/<id>`; `wiring.build_local` as the composition root; ADR 0012.
- `feat(daemon): the read routes` — `GET /decisions`, `/ledger`, `/ledger/rollup`, `/playbooks`, all off read connections; the brain's read helpers moved off the writer; `athena serve`, `doctor`, `brain reconcile`; `docs/daemon.md` with the curl sequence.
- `fix(core): a model cannot answer its own card` — `core.answer_decision` removed from the catalog; the only path to a resolved approval is `POST /decisions/<id>`; ADR 0004 amended.

- `feat(bridge): inject.js, the page's half` — the `document.modelContext` polyfill, list/call over postMessage, toolchange, the 30 s abort, degrade to "no bridge"; ADR 0008.
- `feat(bridge): gate.js, the surface's half` — class derivation that only tightens, the refusal vocabulary pinned to `ERROR_REASONS` by `tests/test_refusal_parity.py`, the fence, bounded output, the per-origin budget; ADR 0009.

## P2 — The gate and the model

- `feat(core): approvals and the ledger` — the durable HITL table with exact-token resolve and 24 h expiry; one ledger row per invocation with normalised error reasons; both survive a reconcile; ADR 0005.
- `feat(core): the constitution and the two-output prompt composer` — law and identity from the wheel, a bundle or a marker-guarded checkout; `StaticBlocks` for the system prompt and a nonce-fenced `TurnFrame` for the user message; ADR 0006.
- `feat(harness): ports, hooks and the OP grammar` — `GateHook` (validator before class, replay proves the grant), `LedgerHook`, `TruncationHook`; the `OP:` parser with three repair rules.
- `feat(harness): the CLI harness in two dialects` — `claude` and `codex` through a `Transport` port, `--resume` for Claude, eight rounds per turn, engine probes; adds the `turn` id kind to `contracts/ids.py`; ADR 0007.

## P1 — Foundation

- `chore: scaffold the repository` — pyproject, uv lock, pnpm workspace, the gate config, ADR 0001.
- `ci: the gate on three Pythons and the JavaScript workspace` — GitHub Actions, 3.11 to 3.13.
- `feat(contracts): the seams every package is written against` — registry, manifest, channel events, harness, ids; ADR 0002 stdlib-only core.
- `feat(core): the brain, disk first, index second` — episodes, facts and procedurals with provenance at write; one writer, a read-only connection per request; reconcile; ADR 0003.
- `feat(core): recall, bounded and announced` — BM25 over FTS5, always-include tiers, a 20-episode window, per-block `(showing N of M)`.
- `feat(core): the catalog is the policy` — three registries, validators, manifest merge, `render_capabilities`, the `ConnectorPort` seam; ADR 0004.
